




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

aṅguttaranikāyau


sattakanipātapāḷi


paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ


1. dhanavaggau

1. paṭhamapiyasuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi sattahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu lābhakāmau  ca hauti, sak‍kārakāmau ca hauti, anavañ‍ñattikāmau ca hauti, ahirikau ca hauti, anauttappī ca, pāpicchau ca, micchādiṭṭhi ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca|

‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti, manāpau ca  garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi sattahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na lābhakāmau ca hauti, na sak‍kārakāmau ca hauti, na anavañ‍ñattikāmau ca hauti, hirimā ca hauti, auttappī ca, appicchau  ca, sammādiṭṭhi ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyapiyasuttaṃ

2. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi sattahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu lābhakāmau ca hauti, sak‍kārakāmau ca hauti, anavañ‍ñattikāmau ca hauti, ahirikau ca hauti, anauttappī ca, issukī ca, maccharī ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca|

‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu+? sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi sattahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na lābhakāmau ca hauti, na sak‍kārakāmau ca hauti, na anavañ‍ñattikāmau ca hauti, hirimā ca hauti, auttappī ca, anissukī ca, amaccharī ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saṃkhittabalasuttaṃ

3. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai…pai॰… sattimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni satta? saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, hirībalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, satta balānīti|

‘‘saddhābalaṃ  vīriyañ‍ca, hirī hiri (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) auttappiyaṃ balaṃ|

satibalaṃ samādhi ca, pañ‍ñā vai sattamaṃ balaṃ|

aitaihi balavā bhikkhu, sukhaṃ jīvati paṇḍitau|

‘‘yaunisau  vicinai dhammaṃ, pañ‍ñāyatthaṃ vipassati|

paj‍jautassaiva nibbānaṃ, vimaukkhau hauti caitasau’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. vitthatabalasuttaṃ

4. ‘‘sattimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni satta? saddhābala, vīriyabalaṃ, hirībalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saddhābalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saddhābalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vīriyabalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vīriyabalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, hirībalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau hirimā hauti, hirīyati kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina, hirīyati pāpakānaṃ  akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, hirībalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, auttappabalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau auttappī hauti, auttappati  kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina, auttappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, auttappabalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, satibalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau  satimā hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| idaṃ, vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, satibalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, samādhibalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, samādhibalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñābalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñābalaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, satta balānīti|

‘‘saddhābalaṃ vīriyañ‍ca, hirī auttappiyaṃ balaṃ|

satibalaṃ samādhi ca, pañ‍ñā vai sattamaṃ balaṃ|

aitaihi balavā bhikkhu, sukhaṃ jīvati paṇḍitau||

‘‘yaunisau vicinai dhammaṃ, pañ‍ñāyatthaṃ vipassati|

paj‍jautassaiva nibbānaṃ, vimaukkhau hauti caitasau’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. saṃkhittadhanasuttaṃ

5. ‘‘sattimāni, bhikkhavai, dhanāni| katamāni satta? saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, hirīdhanaṃ, auttappadhanaṃ, sutadhanaṃ, cāgadhanaṃ, pañ‍ñādhanaṃ| imāni  khau, bhikkhavai, satta dhanānīti|

‘‘saddhādhanaṃ  sīladhanaṃ, hirī auttappiyaṃ dhanaṃ|

sutadhanañ‍ca cāgau ca, pañ‍ñā vai sattamaṃ dhanaṃ||

‘‘yassa aitai dhanā atthi, itthiyā purisassa vā|

adaliddauti taṃ āhu, amaughaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ||

‘‘tasmā  saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ|

anuyuñ‍jaitha maidhāvī, saraṃ varaṃ (ka॰) buddhāna sāsana’’nti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. vitthatadhanasuttaṃ

6. ‘‘sattimāni, bhikkhavai, dhanāni| katamāni satta? saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, hirīdhanaṃ, auttappadhanaṃ, sutadhanaṃ, cāgadhanaṃ, pañ‍ñādhanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saddhādhanaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… buddhau bhagavā’ti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saddhādhanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sīladhanaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sīladhanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, hirīdhanaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau hirīmā hauti, hirīyati kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina, hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, hirīdhanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, auttappadhanaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau auttappī hauti, auttappati kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina, auttappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, auttappadhanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sutadhanaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau| yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti| tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā  suppaṭividdhā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sutadhanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, cāgadhanaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, cāgadhanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñādhanaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pañ‍ñavā hauti…pai॰… sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñādhanaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, sattadhanānīti|

‘‘saddhādhanaṃ sīladhanaṃ, hirī auttappiyaṃ dhanaṃ|

sutadhanañ‍ca cāgau ca, pañ‍ñā vai sattamaṃ dhanaṃ||

‘‘yassa aitai dhanā atthi, itthiyā purisassa vā|

adaliddauti taṃ āhu, amaughaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ||

‘‘tasmā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ|

anuyuñ‍jaitha maidhāvī, saraṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. uggasuttaṃ

7. atha  khau uggau rājamahāmattau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uggau rājamahāmattau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāva aḍḍhau cāyaṃ, bhantai, migārau rauhaṇaiyyau yāva mahaddhanau yāva mahābhaugau’’ti| ‘‘kīva aḍḍhau  panugga, migārau rauhaṇaiyyau, kīva mahaddhanau, kīva mahābhaugau’’ti? ‘‘sataṃ, bhantai, satasahassānaṃ sahassānaṃ (sī॰), sahassāni (syā॰), satasahassāni (?) hirañ‍ñassa, kau pana vādau rūpiyassā’’ti! ‘‘atthi khau aitaṃ, ugga, dhanaṃ naitaṃ ‘natthī’ti vadāmīti| tañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, ugga, dhanaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ agginā udakaina rājūhi cauraihi appiyaihi dāyādaihi| satta khau imāni, ugga, dhanāni asādhāraṇāni agginā udakaina rājūhi cauraihi appiyaihi dāyādaihi| katamāni satta? saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, hirīdhanaṃ, auttappadhanaṃ, sutadhanaṃ, cāgadhanaṃ, pañ‍ñādhanaṃ| imāni khau, ugga, satta dhanāni asādhāraṇāni agginā udakaina rājūhi cauraihi appiyaihi dāyādaihīti|

‘‘saddhādhanaṃ sīladhanaṃ, hirī auttappiyaṃ dhanaṃ|

sutadhanañ‍ca cāgau ca, pañ‍ñā vai sattamaṃ dhanaṃ||

‘‘yassa aitai dhanā atthi, itthiyā purisassa vā|

sa vai mahaddhanau laukai, ajaiyyau daivamānusai||

‘‘tasmā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ|

anuyuñ‍jaitha maidhāvī, saraṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti|| sattamaṃ|

8. saṃyaujanasuttaṃ

8. ‘‘sattimāni, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujanāni| katamāni satta? anunayasaṃyaujanaṃ, paṭighasaṃyaujanaṃ, diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaṃ, vicikicchāsaṃyaujanaṃ, mānasaṃyaujanaṃ, bhavarāgasaṃyaujanaṃ, avij‍jāsaṃyaujanaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, satta saṃyaujanānī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pahānasuttaṃ

9. ‘‘sattan‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati| katamaisaṃ  sattan‍naṃ? anunayasaṃyaujanassa  pahānāya samucchaidāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati, paṭighasaṃyaujanassa…pai॰… diṭṭhisaṃyaujanassa… vicikicchāsaṃyaujanassa… mānasaṃyaujanassa… bhavarāgasaṃyaujanassa … avij‍jāsaṃyaujanassa pahānāya samucchaidāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sattan‍naṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau anunayasaṃyaujanaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃ kataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ| paṭighasaṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaṃ… vicikicchāsaṃyaujanaṃ… mānasaṃyaujanaṃ… bhavarāgasaṃyaujanaṃ… avij‍jāsaṃyaujanaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃ kataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu acchaicchi taṇhaṃ, vivattayi saṃyaujanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. macchariyasuttaṃ

10. ‘‘sattimāni, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujanāni| katamāni satta? anunayasaṃyaujanaṃ, paṭighasaṃyaujanaṃ, diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaṃ, vicikicchāsaṃyaujanaṃ, mānasaṃyaujanaṃ, issāsaṃyaujanaṃ, macchariyasaṃyaujanaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, satta saṃyaujanānī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

dhanavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai  piyāni balaṃ dhanaṃ, saṃkhittañ‍caiva vitthataṃ|

uggaṃ saṃyaujanañ‍caiva, pahānaṃ macchariyaina cāti||


2. anusayavaggau

1. paṭhamaanusayasuttaṃ

11. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, anusayā| katamai satta? kāmarāgānusayau, paṭighānusayau, diṭṭhānusayau, vicikicchānusayau, mānānusayau, bhavarāgānusayau, avij‍jānusayau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta anusayā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyaanusayasuttaṃ

12. ‘‘sattan‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, anusayānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati| katamaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ? kāmarāgānusayassa pahānāya samucchaidāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati, paṭighānusayassa…pai॰… diṭṭhānusayassa… vicikicchānusayassa… mānānusayassa… bhavarāgānusayassa… avij‍jānusayassa pahānāya samucchaidāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sattan‍naṃ anusayānaṃ pahānāya samucchaidāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kāmarāgānusayau pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| paṭighānusayau…pai॰… diṭṭhānusayau… vicikicchānusayau… mānānusayau… bhavarāgānusayau… avij‍jānusayau pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu acchaicchi taṇhaṃ, vivattayi saṃyaujanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. kulasuttaṃ

13. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā nālaṃ upagantuṃ, upagantvā  vā nālaṃ upanisīdituṃ| katamaihi sattahi? na manāpaina pac‍cuṭṭhainti, na manāpaina abhivādainti, na manāpaina āsanaṃ dainti, santamassa pariguhanti, bahukampi thaukaṃ dainti, paṇītampi lūkhaṃ dainti, asak‍kac‍caṃ dainti nau sak‍kac‍caṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā nālaṃ upagantuṃ, upagantvā vā  nālaṃ upanisīdituṃ|

‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā alaṃ upagantuṃ, upagantvā vā alaṃ upanisīdituṃ| katamaihi sattahi? manāpaina pac‍cuṭṭhainti, manāpaina abhivādainti, manāpaina āsanaṃ dainti, santamassa na pariguhanti, bahukampi bahukaṃ dainti, paṇītampi paṇītaṃ dainti, sak‍kac‍caṃ dainti nau asak‍kac‍caṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā alaṃ upagantuṃ, upagantvā vā alaṃ upanisīditu’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. puggalasuttaṃ

14. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā āhunaiyyā pāhunaiyyā dakkhiṇaiyyā añ‍jalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamai satta? ubhataubhāgavimuttau, pañ‍ñāvimuttau, kāyasakkhī, diṭṭhippattau diṭṭhappattau (ka॰), saddhāvimuttau, dhammānusārī, saddhānusārī| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, satta puggalā āhunaiyyā pāhunaiyyā dakkhiṇaiyyā añ‍jalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. udakūpamāsuttaṃ

15. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, udakūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai satta? pu॰ pa॰ 203; kathā॰ 852 idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sakiṃ nimuggau nimuggauva hauti; idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ummuj‍jitvā nimuj‍jati; idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ummuj‍jitvā ṭhitau hauti; idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ummuj‍jitvā vipassati vilaukaiti; idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ummuj‍jitvā patarati; idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau  puggalau ummuj‍jitvā patigādhappattau hauti; idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ummuj‍jitvā tiṇṇau hauti pāraṅgatau pāragatau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau sakiṃ nimuggau nimuggauva hauti? idha bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau saman‍nāgatau hauti aikantakāḷakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau sakiṃ nimuggau nimuggauva hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau ummuj‍jitvā nimuj‍jati? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ummuj‍jati sādhu saddhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, sādhu hirī…pai॰… sādhu auttappaṃ… sādhu vīriyaṃ viriyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) … sādhu pañ‍ñā kusalaisu dhammaisūti| tassa sā saddhā naiva tiṭṭhati nau vaḍḍhati hāyatiyaiva, tassa sā hirī…pai॰… tassa taṃ auttappaṃ… tassa taṃ vīriyaṃ… tassa sā pañ‍ñā naiva tiṭṭhati nau vaḍḍhati hāyatiyaiva| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau ummuj‍jitvā nimuj‍jati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau ummuj‍jitvā ṭhitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ummuj‍jati sādhu saddhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, sādhu hirī…pai॰… sādhu auttappaṃ… sādhu vīriyaṃ… sādhu pañ‍ñā kusalaisu dhammaisūti| tassa sā saddhā naiva hāyati nau vaḍḍhati ṭhitā hauti| tassa sā hirī…pai॰… tassa taṃ auttappaṃ… tassa taṃ vīriyaṃ… tassa sā pañ‍ñā naiva hāyati nau vaḍḍhati ṭhitā hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau ummuj‍jitvā ṭhitau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau ummuj‍jitvā vipassati vilaukaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ummuj‍jati sādhu saddhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, sādhu hirī…pai॰… sādhu auttappaṃ… sādhu vīriyaṃ… sādhu pañ‍ñā kusalaisu dhammaisūti| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nau  hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau ummuj‍jitvā vipassati vilaukaiti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau ummuj‍jitvā patarati? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ummuj‍jati  sādhu saddhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, sādhu hirī…pai॰… sādhu auttappaṃ… sādhu vīriyaṃ… sādhu pañ‍ñā kusalaisu dhammaisūti| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hauti, sakidaiva sakiṃdaiva (ka॰) imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti dukkhassantakarau hauti (ka॰)| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau ummuj‍jitvā patarati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau ummuj‍jitvā patigādhappattau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ummuj‍jati sādhu saddhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, sādhu hirī…pai॰… sādhu auttappaṃ… sādhu vīriyaṃ… sādhu pañ‍ñā kusalaisu dhammaisūti| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau ummuj‍jitvā patigādhappattau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau ummuj‍jitvā tiṇṇau hauti pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau| idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ummuj‍jati sādhu saddhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, sādhu hirī…pai॰… sādhu auttappaṃ… sādhu vīriyaṃ… sādhu pañ‍ñā  kusalaisu dhammaisūti| sau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau ummuj‍jitvā tiṇṇau hauti pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau|

‘‘imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta udakūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. anic‍cānupassīsuttaṃ

16. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā āhunaiyyā pāhunaiyyā dakkhiṇaiyyā añ‍jalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamai satta? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassī viharati, anic‍casañ‍ñī, anic‍capaṭisaṃvaidī satataṃ samitaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ caitasā adhimuc‍camānau pañ‍ñāya pariyaugāhamānau| sau āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā  upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau puggalau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassī viharati, anic‍casañ‍ñī, anic‍capaṭisaṃvaidī satataṃ samitaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ caitasā adhimuc‍camānau pañ‍ñāya pariyaugāhamānau| tassa apubbaṃ acarimaṃ āsavapariyādānañ‍ca hauti jīvitapariyādānañ‍ca| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau puggalau āhunaiyyau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassī viharati, anic‍casañ‍ñī, anic‍capaṭisaṃvaidī satataṃ samitaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ caitasā adhimuc‍camānau pañ‍ñāya pariyaugāhamānau| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ  saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattamau puggalau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta puggalā āhunaiyyā  pāhunaiyyā dakkhiṇaiyyā añ‍jalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dukkhānupassīsuttaṃ

17. sattimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā āhunaiyyā…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamai satta? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sabbasaṅkhāraisu dukkhānupassī viharati…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. anattānupassīsuttaṃ

18. sabbaisu dhammaisu anattānupassī viharati…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. nibbānasuttaṃ

19. kathā॰ 547-548 ‘‘nibbānai  sukhānupassī viharati sukhasañ‍ñī sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī satataṃ samitaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ caitasā adhimuc‍camānau pañ‍ñāya pariyaugāhamānau| sau āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ bhikkhavai, paṭhamau puggalau āhunaiyyau…pai॰… puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau nibbānai sukhānupassī viharati sukhasañ‍ñī sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī satataṃ samitaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ caitasā adhimuc‍camānau pañ‍ñāya pariyaugāhamānau| tassa apubbaṃ acarimaṃ āsavapariyādānañ‍ca hauti jīvitapariyādānañ‍ca| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau puggalau āhunaiyyau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau nibbānai sukhānupassī viharati sukhasañ‍ñī sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī satataṃ samitaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ caitasā adhimuc‍camānau pañ‍ñāya pariyaugāhamānau| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… upahac‍caparinibbāyī  hauti…pai॰… asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattamau puggalau āhunaiyyau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta puggalā āhunaiyyā…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. niddasavatthusuttaṃ

20. ‘‘sattimāni, bhikkhavai, niddasavatthūni| katamāni satta? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sikkhāsamādānai tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca sikkhāsamādānai avigatapaimau adhigatapaimau (syā॰) a॰ ni॰ 7.42; dī॰ ni॰ 3.331, dhammanisantiyā tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca dhammanisantiyā avigatapaimau, icchāvinayai tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca icchāvinayai avigatapaimau, paṭisal‍lānai tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca paṭisal‍lānai avigatapaimau, vīriyārambhai vīriyārabbhai (ka॰) tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca vīriyārambhai avigatapaimau, satinaipak‍kai tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca satinaipak‍kai avigatapaimau, diṭṭhipaṭivaidhai  tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca diṭṭhipaṭivaidhai avigatapaimau| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, satta niddasavatthūnī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

anusayavaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

duvai anusayā kulaṃ, puggalaṃ udakūpamaṃ|

anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ anattā ca, nibbānaṃ niddasavatthu cāti||


3. vaj‍jisattakavaggau

1. sārandadasuttaṃ

21. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati sārandadai caitiyai| atha khau sambahulā licchavī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai licchavī  bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘satta vau, licchavī, aparihāniyai aparihānīyai (ka॰) dhammai daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai licchavī bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamai  ca, licchavī, satta aparihāniyā dhammā? yāvakīvañ‍ca, licchavī, vaj‍jī abhiṇhaṃ san‍nipātā bhavissanti san‍nipātabahulā; vuddhiyaiva, licchavī, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, licchavī, vaj‍jī samaggā san‍nipatissanti, samaggā vuṭṭhahissanti, samaggā vaj‍jikaraṇīyāni karissanti; vuddhiyaiva, licchavī, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, licchavī, vaj‍jī apañ‍ñattaṃ na pañ‍ñāpaissanti, pañ‍ñattaṃ na samucchindissanti, yathāpañ‍ñattai paurāṇai vaj‍jidhammai samādāya vattissanti; vuddhiyaiva, licchavī, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, licchavī, vaj‍jī yai tai vaj‍jīnaṃ vaj‍jimahal‍lakā tai sak‍karissanti garuṃ karissanti mānaissanti pūjaissanti, taisañ‍ca sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñissanti; vuddhiyaiva, licchavī, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, licchavī, vaj‍jī yā tā kulitthiyau kulakumāriyau tā na aukassa pasayha vāsaissanti; vuddhiyaiva, licchavī, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, licchavī, vaj‍jī yāni tāni vaj‍jīnaṃ vaj‍jicaitiyāni abbhantarāni caiva bāhirāni ca tāni sak‍karissanti garuṃ karissanti mānaissanti  pūjaissanti, taisañ‍ca din‍napubbaṃ katapubbaṃ dhammikaṃ baliṃ nau parihāpaissanti; vuddhiyaiva, licchavī, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, licchavī, vaj‍jīnaṃ arahantaisu dhammikā  rakkhāvaraṇagutti susaṃvihitā bhavissati – ‘kinti anāgatā ca arahantau vijitaṃ āgacchaiyyuṃ, āgatā ca arahantau vijitai phāsuṃ viharaiyyu’nti; vuddhiyaiva, licchavī, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, licchavī, imai satta aparihāniyā dhammā vaj‍jīsu ṭhassanti vattissanti (ka॰), imaisu ca sattasu aparihāniyaisu dhammaisu vaj‍jī sandississanti sandissanti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰); vuddhiyaiva, licchavī, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihānī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. vassakārasuttaṃ

22. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau vaj‍jī abhiyātukāmau hauti| sau aivamāha – ‘‘ahaṃ himai vaj‍jī aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai ucchaicchāmi ucchaij‍jissāmi (syā॰), ucchij‍jissāmi (ka॰), vaj‍jī vināsaissāmi, vaj‍jī anayabyasanaṃ āpādaissāmī’’ti āpādaissāmi vaj‍jīti (ka॰) dī॰ ni॰ 2.131|

atha khau rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau vassakāraṃ brāhmaṇaṃ māgadhamahāmattaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandāhi, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ puccha – ‘rājā, bhantai, māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘rājā, bhantai, māgadhau ajātasattu  vaidaihiputtau vaj‍jī abhiyātukāmau| sau aivamāha – ‘ahaṃ himai vaj‍jī aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai ucchaicchāmi, vaj‍jī vināsaissāmi, vaj‍jī anayabyasanaṃ āpādaissāmī’ti| yathā  tai bhagavā byākarauti, taṃ sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā mama āraucaiyyāsi| na hi tathāgatā vitathaṃ bhaṇantī’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau māgadhamahāmattau rañ‍ñau māgadhassa ajātasattussa vaidaihiputtassa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau māgadhamahāmattau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘rājā, bhau gautama, māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau bhautau gautamassa pādai sirasā vandati, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchati| rājā aivañ‍ca vadaiti rājā (sī॰ ka॰), bhau gautama, māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau vaj‍jī abhiyātukāmau| sau aivamāha – ‘ahaṃ himai vaj‍jī aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai ucchaicchāmi, vaj‍jī vināsaissāmi, vaj‍jī anayabyasanaṃ āpādaissāmī’’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau piṭṭhitau ṭhitau hauti bhagavantaṃ bījayamānau vījamānau (sī॰ syā॰)| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kinti tai, ānanda, sutaṃ – ‘vaj‍jī abhiṇhaṃ san‍nipātā san‍nipātabahulā’’’ti? ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘vaj‍jī abhiṇhaṃ san‍nipātā san‍nipātabahulā’’’ti| ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, ānanda, vaj‍jī abhiṇhaṃ san‍nipātā bhavissanti san‍nipātabahulā; vuddhiyaiva, ānanda, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘kinti tai, ānanda, sutaṃ – ‘vaj‍jī samaggā san‍nipatanti, samaggā vuṭṭhahanti, samaggā vaj‍jikaraṇīyāni karauntī’’’ti? ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘vaj‍jī samaggā san‍nipatanti, samaggā vuṭṭhahanti, samaggā vaj‍jikaraṇīyāni karauntī’’’ti| ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, ānanda, vaj‍jī samaggā san‍nipatissanti, samaggā vuṭṭhahissanti, samaggā vaj‍jikaraṇīyāni karissanti; vuddhiyaiva, ānanda, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘kinti tai, ānanda, sutaṃ – ‘vaj‍jī apañ‍ñattaṃ na pañ‍ñāpainti, pañ‍ñattaṃ na samucchindanti, yathāpañ‍ñattai paurāṇai vaj‍jidhammai samādāya vattantī’’’ti? ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘vaj‍jī apañ‍ñattaṃ na pañ‍ñāpainti, pañ‍ñattaṃ na samucchindanti, yathāpañ‍ñattai paurāṇai vaj‍jidhammai samādāya vattantī’’’ti| ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, ānanda, vaj‍jī apañ‍ñattaṃ na pañ‍ñāpaissanti, pañ‍ñattaṃ na samucchindissanti, yathāpañ‍ñattai paurāṇai vaj‍jidhammai samādāya vattissanti; vuddhiyaiva, ānanda, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘kinti tai, ānanda, sutaṃ – ‘vaj‍jī yai tai vaj‍jīnaṃ vaj‍jimahal‍lakā tai sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, taisañ‍ca sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñantī’’’ti? ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘vaj‍jī yai tai vaj‍jīnaṃ vaj‍jimahal‍lakā tai sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, taisañ‍ca sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñantī’’’ti| ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, ānanda, vaj‍jī yai tai vaj‍jīnaṃ vaj‍jimahal‍lakā tai sak‍karissanti garuṃ karissanti mānaissanti pūjaissanti, taisañ‍ca sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñissanti; vuddhiyaiva, ānanda, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘kinti tai, ānanda, sutaṃ – ‘vaj‍jī yā tā kulitthiyau kulakumāriyau tā na aukassa pasayha vāsaintī’’’ti? ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘vaj‍jī yā tā kulitthiyau kulakumāriyau tā na aukassa pasayha vāsaintī’’’ti| ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, ānanda, vaj‍jī yā tā kulitthiyau  kulakumāriyau tā na aukassa pasayha vāsaissanti; vuddhiyaiva, ānanda, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘kinti tai, ānanda, sutaṃ – ‘vaj‍jī yāni tāni vaj‍jīnaṃ vaj‍jicaitiyāni abbhantarāni caiva bāhirāni ca tāni sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, taisañ‍ca din‍napubbaṃ katapubbaṃ dhammikaṃ baliṃ nau parihāpaintī’’’ti? ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘vaj‍jī yāni tāni vaj‍jīnaṃ vaj‍jicaitiyāni abbhantarāni caiva bāhirāni ca tāni sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, taisañ‍ca din‍napubbaṃ katapubbaṃ dhammikaṃ baliṃ nau parihāpaintī’’’ti| ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, ānanda, vaj‍jī yāni  tāni vaj‍jīnaṃ vaj‍jicaitiyāni abbhantarāni caiva bāhirāni ca tāni sak‍karissanti garuṃ karissanti mānaissanti pūjaissanti, taisañ‍ca din‍napubbaṃ katapubbaṃ dhammikaṃ baliṃ nau parihāpaissanti; vuddhiyaiva, ānanda, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘kinti  tai, ānanda, sutaṃ – ‘vaj‍jīnaṃ arahantaisu dhammikā rakkhāvaraṇagutti susaṃvihitā – kinti anāgatā ca arahantau vijitaṃ āgacchaiyyuṃ, āgatā ca arahantau vijitai phāsuṃ viharaiyyu’’’nti? ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘vaj‍jīnaṃ arahantaisu dhammikā rakkhāvaraṇagutti susaṃvihitā bhavissati – kinti anāgatā ca arahantau vijitaṃ āgacchaiyyuṃ, āgatā ca arahantau vijitai phāsuṃ viharaiyyu’’’nti| ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, ānanda, vaj‍jīnaṃ arahantaisu dhammikā rakkhāvaraṇagutti susaṃvihitā bhavissati – ‘kinti anāgatā ca arahantau vijitaṃ āgacchaiyyuṃ, āgatā ca arahantau vijitai phāsuṃ viharaiyyu’nti; vuddhiyaiva, ānanda, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihānī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā vassakāraṃ brāhmaṇaṃ māgadhamahāmattaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, samayaṃ vaisāliyaṃ viharāmi sārandadai caitiyai| tatrāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, vaj‍jīnaṃ imai satta aparihāniyai dhammai daisaisiṃ| yāvakīvañ‍ca, brāhmaṇa, imai satta aparihāniyā dhammā vaj‍jīsu ṭhassanti, imaisu ca sattasu aparihāniyaisu dhammaisu vaj‍jī sandississanti; vuddhiyaiva, brāhmaṇa, vaj‍jīnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihānī’’ti|

‘‘aikamaikainapi  aikamaikainapi taina khau (ka॰) dī॰ ni॰ 2.135 bhau, gautama, aparihāniyaina dhammaina saman‍nāgatānaṃ vaj‍jīnaṃ vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni; kau pana vādau sattahi aparihāniyaihi dhammaihi! akaraṇīyā ca, bhau gautama, vaj‍jī rañ‍ñā māgadhaina ajātasattunā vaidaihiputtaina yadidaṃ yuddhassa, añ‍ñatra upalāpanāya  upalāpanā (ka॰ sī॰ ka॰), añ‍ñatra mithubhaidā| handa ca dāni mayaṃ, bhau gautama, gacchāma, bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau māgadhamahāmattau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmīti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamasattakasuttaṃ

23. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘satta vau, bhikkhavai, aparihāniyai dhammai daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, satta aparihāniyā dhammā? yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū  abhiṇhaṃ san‍nipātā bhavissanti san‍nipātabahulā; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū samaggā san‍nipatissanti, samaggā vuṭṭhahissanti, samaggā saṅghakaraṇīyāni karissanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū apañ‍ñattaṃ na pañ‍ñāpaissanti, pañ‍ñattaṃ na samucchindissanti, yathāpañ‍ñattaisu sikkhāpadaisu samādāya vattissanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū yai tai bhikkhū thairā rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitā saṅghapitarau saṅghapariṇāyakā tai sak‍karissanti garuṃ karissanti mānaissanti pūjaissanti, taisañ‍ca sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñissanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū uppan‍nāya taṇhāya paunaubhavikāya na vasaṃ gacchissanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu sāpaikkhā bhavissanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva satiṃ upaṭṭhāpaissanti – ‘kinti anāgatā ca paisalā sabrahmacārī āgacchaiyyuṃ, āgatā ca paisalā sabrahmacārī phāsuṃ viharaiyyu’nti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, imai satta aparihāniyā dhammā bhikkhūsu ṭhassanti, imaisu ca sattasu aparihāniyaisu dhammaisu bhikkhū sandississanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ  pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihānī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyasattakasuttaṃ

24. dī॰ ni॰ 2.138 ‘‘satta vau, bhikkhavai, aparihāniyai dhammai daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha…pai॰… katamai ca, bhikkhavai, satta aparihāniyā dhammā?

yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū na kammārāmā bhavissanti, na kammaratā, na kammārāmataṃ anuyuttā; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū na bhassārāmā bhavissanti…pai॰… na niddārāmā bhavissanti… na saṅgaṇikārāmā bhavissanti… na pāpicchā bhavissanti na pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā… na pāpamittā bhavissanti na pāpasahāyā na pāpasampavaṅkā… na auramattakaina visaisādhigamaina antarāvausānaṃ āpaj‍jissanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, imai satta aparihāniyā dhammā bhikkhūsu ṭhassanti, imaisu ca sattasu aparihāniyaisu dhammaisu bhikkhū sandississanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihānī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. tatiyasattakasuttaṃ

25. ‘‘satta  vau, bhikkhavai, aparihāniyai dhammai daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha…pai॰… katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, satta aparihāniyā dhammā? yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū saddhā bhavissanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū hirimantau hirīmā (sī॰), hirimanā (dī॰ ni॰ 2.138) bhavissanti…pai॰… auttappinau auttāpīnau (sī॰) bhavissanti… bahussutā bhavissanti… āraddhavīriyā bhavissanti… satimantau bhavissanti… pañ‍ñavantau bhavissanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni| ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, imai satta aparihāniyā  dhammā bhikkhūsu ṭhassanti, imaisu ca sattasu aparihāniyaisu dhammaisu bhikkhū sandississanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. baujjhaṅgasuttaṃ

26. ‘‘satta vau, bhikkhavai, aparihāniyai dhammai daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha…pai॰… katamai ca, bhikkhavai, satta aparihāniyā dhammā? yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaissanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaissanti…pai॰… vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaissanti… pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaissanti… passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaissanti… samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaissanti… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaissanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni| ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, imai satta aparihāniyā dhammā bhikkhūsu ṭhassanti, imaisu ca sattasu aparihāniyaisu dhammaisu bhikkhū sandississanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihānī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

27. ‘‘satta  vau, bhikkhavai, aparihāniyai dhammai daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha…pai॰…| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, satta aparihāniyā  dhammā? yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū anic‍casañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaissanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū anattasañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaissanti…pai॰… asubhasañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaissanti… ādīnavasañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaissanti… pahānasañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaissanti… virāgasañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaissanti… niraudhasañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaissanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni| ‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, imai satta aparihāniyā dhammā bhikkhūsu ṭhassanti, imaisu ca sattasu aparihāniyaisu dhammaisu, bhikkhū sandississanti; vuddhiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihānī’’ti dī॰ ni॰ 2.138| sattamaṃ|

8. paṭhamaparihānisuttaṃ

28. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā, saṅgaṇikārāmatā, indriyaisu aguttadvāratā, bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutā, santi khau pana saṅghai saṅghakaraṇīyāni; tatra saikhau bhikkhu tatra bhikkhu (sī॰ syā॰) iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘santi khau pana saṅghai thairā khau saṃghatthairā (ka॰) rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitā bhāravāhinau, tai na tai (ka॰) taina pañ‍ñāyissantī’ti attanā taisu yaugaṃ attanā vauyaugaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) āpaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? na  kammārāmatā, na bhassārāmatā, na niddārāmatā, na saṅgaṇikārāmatā, indriyaisu guttadvāratā, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā, santi khau pana saṅghai saṅghakaraṇīyāni; tatra saikhau bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘santi khau pana saṅghai thairā rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitā bhāravāhinau, tai taina pañ‍ñāyissantī’ti attanā na taisu yaugaṃ āpaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyaparihānisuttaṃ

29. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā upāsakassa parihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? bhikkhudassanaṃ hāpaiti, saddhammassavanaṃ pamaj‍jati, adhisīlai na sikkhati, appasādabahulau hauti, bhikkhūsu thairaisu caiva navaisu ca majjhimaisu ca upārambhacittau dhammaṃ suṇāti randhagavaisī, itau bahiddhā dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ gavaisati, tattha ca pubbakāraṃ karauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta dhammā upāsakassa parihānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā upāsakassa aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? bhikkhudassanaṃ na hāpaiti, saddhammassavanaṃ nappamaj‍jati, adhisīlai sikkhati, pasādabahulau hauti, bhikkhūsu thairaisu caiva navaisu ca majjhimaisu ca anupārambhacittau dhammaṃ suṇāti na randhagavaisī, na itau bahiddhā dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ gavaisati, idha ca pubbakāraṃ karauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta dhammā upāsakassa aparihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘dassanaṃ  bhāvitattānaṃ, yau hāpaiti upāsakau|

savanañ‍ca ariyadhammānaṃ, adhisīlai na sikkhati||

‘‘appasādau ca bhikkhūsu, bhiyyau bhiyyau pavaḍḍhati|

upārambhakacittau ca, saddhammaṃ sautumicchati||

‘‘itau ca bahiddhā añ‍ñaṃ, dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ gavaisati|

tatthaiva ca pubbakāraṃ, yau karauti upāsakau||

‘‘aitai khau parihāniyai, satta dhammai sudaisitai|

upāsakau saivamānau, saddhammā parihāyati||

‘‘dassanaṃ bhāvitattānaṃ, yau na hāpaiti upāsakau|

savanañ‍ca ariyadhammānaṃ, adhisīlai ca sikkhati||

‘‘pasādau cassa bhikkhūsu, bhiyyau bhiyyau pavaḍḍhati|

anupārambhacittau ca, saddhammaṃ sautumicchati||

‘‘na  itau bahiddhā añ‍ñaṃ, dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ gavaisati|

idhaiva ca pubbakāraṃ, yau karauti upāsakau||

‘‘aitai  khau aparihāniyai, satta dhammai sudaisitai|

upāsakau saivamānau, saddhammā na parihāyatī’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. vipattisuttaṃ

30. sattimā, bhikkhavai, upāsakassa vipattiyau…pai॰… sattimā, bhikkhavai, upāsakassa sampadā…pai॰…| dasamaṃ|

11. parābhavasuttaṃ

31. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, upāsakassa parābhavā…pai॰… sattimai, bhikkhavai, upāsakassa sambhavā| katamai satta? bhikkhudassanaṃ na hāpaiti, saddhammassavanaṃ nappamaj‍jati, adhisīlai sikkhati, pasādabahulau hauti, bhikkhūsu thairaisu caiva navaisu ca majjhimaisu ca anupārambhacittau dhammaṃ suṇāti na randhagavaisī, na itau bahiddhā dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ gavaisati, idha ca pubbakāraṃ karauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta upāsakassa sambhavāti|

‘‘dassanaṃ  bhāvitattānaṃ, yau hāpaiti upāsakau|

savanañ‍ca ariyadhammānaṃ, adhisīlai na sikkhati||

‘‘appasādau ca bhikkhūsu, bhiyyau bhiyyau pavaḍḍhati|

upārambhakacittau ca, saddhammaṃ sautumicchati||

‘‘itau ca bahiddhā añ‍ñaṃ, dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ gavaisati|

tatthaiva ca pubbakāraṃ, yau karauti upāsakau||

‘‘aitai  khau parihāniyai, satta dhammai sudaisitai|

upāsakau saivamānau, saddhammā parihāyati||

‘‘dassanaṃ  bhāvitattānaṃ, yau na hāpaiti upāsakau|

savanañ‍ca ariyadhammānaṃ, adhisīlai ca sikkhati||

‘‘pasādau cassa bhikkhūsu, bhiyyau bhiyyau pavaḍḍhati|

anupārambhacittau ca, saddhammaṃ sautumicchati||

‘‘na  itau bahiddhā añ‍ñaṃ, dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ gavaisati|

idhaiva ca pubbakāraṃ, yau karauti upāsakau||

‘‘aitai khau aparihāniyai, satta dhammai sudaisitai|

upāsakau saivamānau, saddhammā na parihāyatī’’ti|| aikādasamaṃ|

vaj‍jisattakavaggau vaj‍jivaggau (syā॰) tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sāranda  -vassakārau ca, tisattakāni bhikkhukā|

baudhisañ‍ñā dvai ca hāni, vipatti ca parābhavauti||


4. daivatāvaggau

1. appamādagāravasuttaṃ

32. atha  khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sattimai, bhantai, dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, samādhigāravatā, appamādagāravatā, paṭisanthāragāravatā| imai khau, bhantai, satta dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| idamavauca sā daivatā| samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| atha khau sā daivatā ‘‘samanuñ‍ñau mai satthā’’ti bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

atha khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘sattimai, bhantai, dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, samādhigāravatā, appamādagāravatā, paṭisanthāragāravatā – imai khau, bhantai, satta dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

‘‘satthugaru dhammagaru, saṅghai ca tibbagāravau|

samādhigaru ātāpī samādhigāravatāpi ca (ka॰), sikkhāya tibbagāravau||

‘‘appamādagaru  bhikkhu, paṭisanthāragāravau|

abhabbau parihānāya, nibbānassaiva santikai’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. hirīgāravasuttaṃ

33. ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘sattimai, bhantai, dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, samādhigāravatā, hirigāravatā, auttappagāravatā| imai khau, bhantai, satta dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

‘‘satthugaru dhammagaru, saṅghai ca tibbagāravau|

samādhigaru ātāpī, sikkhāya tibbagāravau||

‘‘hiri auttappasampan‍nau, sappatissau sagāravau|

abhabbau parihānāya, nibbānassaiva santikai’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamasauvacassatāsuttaṃ

34. ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ añ‍ñatarā daivatā…pai॰… maṃ aitadavauca – ‘sattimai, bhantai, dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, samādhigāravatā, sauvacassatā, kalyāṇamittatā| imai khau, bhantai, satta dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

‘‘satthugaru  dhammagaru, saṅghai ca tibbagāravau|

samādhigaru ātāpī, sikkhāya tibbagāravau||

‘‘kalyāṇamittau suvacau, sappatissau sagāravau|

abhabbau parihānāya, nibbānassaiva santikai’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyasauvacassatāsuttaṃ

35. ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā…pai॰… ‘sattimai, bhantai, dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai satta? satthugāravatā, dhammagāravatā, saṅghagāravatā, sikkhāgāravatā, samādhigāravatā, sauvacassatā, kalyāṇamittatā| imai khau, bhantai, satta dhammā bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, sā daivatā| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imassa khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| idha, bhantai, bhikkhu attanā ca satthugāravau hauti, satthugāravatāya ca vaṇṇavādī| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū na satthugāravā tai ca satthugāravatāya samādapaiti| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū satthugāravā taisañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| attanā ca dhammagāravau hauti…pai॰… saṅghagāravau hauti… sikkhāgāravau hauti… samādhigāravau hauti… suvacau hauti… kalyāṇamittau hauti, kalyāṇamittatāya ca  vaṇṇavādī| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū na kalyāṇamittā tai ca kalyāṇamittatāya samādapaiti| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū kalyāṇamittā taisañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaināti| imassa khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! sādhu khau tvaṃ, sāriputta, imassa mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| idha, sāriputta, bhikkhu attanā ca satthugāravau hauti, satthugāravatāya ca vaṇṇavādī| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū na satthugāravā tai ca satthugāravatāya samādapaiti| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū satthugāravā taisañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| attanā ca dhammagāravau hauti…pai॰… saṅghagāravau hauti… sikkhāgāravau hauti… samādhigāravau hauti… suvacau hauti… kalyāṇamittau hauti, kalyāṇamittatāya ca vaṇṇavādī| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū na kalyāṇamittā tai ca kalyāṇamittatāya samādapaiti| yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū kalyāṇamittā taisañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaināti| imassa khau, sāriputta, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamamittasuttaṃ

36. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mittau saivitabbau| katamaihi sattahi? duddadaṃ dadāti, duk‍karaṃ karauti, dukkhamaṃ khamati, guyhamassa guyhassa (ka॰) āvi karauti, guyhamassa guyhaṃ assa (sī॰), guyhassa (ka॰) pariguhati parigūhati (sī॰ syā॰), pariguyhati (ka॰), āpadāsu na jahati, khīṇaina khīṇai (ka॰) nātimañ‍ñati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mittau saivitabbau’’ti|

‘‘duddadaṃ dadāti mittau, duk‍karañ‍cāpi kubbati|

athaupissa duruttāni, khamati dukkhamāni ca dukkhamānipi (sī॰ syā॰)||

‘‘guyhañ‍ca tassa guyhamassa ca (syā॰) akkhāti, guyhassa parigūhati|

āpadāsu na jahāti, khīṇaina nātimañ‍ñati||

‘‘yamhi aitāni ṭhānāni, saṃvij‍jantīdha saṃvij‍janti ca (ka॰) puggalai|

sau mittau mittakāmaina, bhajitabbau tathāvidhau’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyamittasuttaṃ

37. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu mittau saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau api panuj‍jamānainapi paṇuj‍jamānainapi (sī॰)| katamaihi sattahi? piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca vattā ca vacanakkhamau ca gambhīrañ‍ca kathaṃ kattā hauti, nau ca aṭṭhānai niyaujaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu mittau saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau api panuj‍jamānainapī’’ti|

‘‘piyau garu bhāvanīyau, vattā ca vacanakkhamau|

gambhīrañ‍ca kathaṃ kattā, nau caṭṭhānai niyaujakau niyaujayai (sī॰ syā॰)||

‘‘yamhi aitāni ṭhānāni, saṃvij‍jantīdha puggalai|

sau mittau mittakāmaina, atthakāmānukampatau|

api nāsiyamānaina, bhajitabbau tathāvidhau’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamapaṭisambhidāsuttaṃ

38. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nacirassaiva catassau  paṭisambhidā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya| katamaihi sattahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ mai caitasau līnatta’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘ajjhattaṃ mai saṃkhittaṃ citta’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; bahiddhā vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘bahiddhā mai vikkhittaṃ citta’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; tassa viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti; viditā sañ‍ñā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā  abbhatthaṃ gacchanti; viditā vitak‍kā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti; sappāyāsappāyaisu khau panassa dhammaisu hīnappaṇītaisu kaṇhasuk‍kasappatibhāgaisu nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ hauti sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ suppaṭividdhaṃ pañ‍ñāya| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nacirassaiva catassau paṭisambhidā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyapaṭisambhidāsuttaṃ

39. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sāriputtau catassau paṭisambhidā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| katamaihi sattahi? idha, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau ‘idaṃ  mai caitasau līnatta’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ajjhattaṃ saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘ajjhattaṃ mai saṃkhittaṃ citta’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; bahiddhā vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘bahiddhā mai vikkhittaṃ citta’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; tassa viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti; viditā  sañ‍ñā…pai॰… vitak‍kā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti; sappāyāsappāyaisu khau panassa dhammaisu hīnappaṇītaisu kaṇhasuk‍kasappatibhāgaisu nimittaṃ suggahitaṃ sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ suppaṭividdhaṃ pañ‍ñāya| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sāriputtau catassau paṭisambhidā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamavasasuttaṃ

40. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu cittaṃ vasai vasaṃ (ka॰) vattaiti, nau ca bhikkhu cittassa vasaina vattati| katamaihi sattahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samādhikusalau hauti, samādhissa samāpattikusalau hauti, samādhissa ṭhitikusalau hauti, samādhissa vuṭṭhānakusalau hauti, samādhissa kalyāṇakusalau hauti, samādhissa gaucarakusalau hauti, samādhissa abhinīhārakusalau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu cittaṃ vasai vattaiti, nau ca bhikkhu cittassa vasaina vattatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyavasasuttaṃ

41. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sāriputtau cittaṃ vasai vattaiti, nau ca sāriputtau cittassa vasaina vattati| katamaihi sattahi? idha, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau samādhikusalau hauti, samādhissa samāpattikusalau, samādhissa ṭhitikusalau, samādhissa vuṭṭhānakusalau, samādhissa kalyāṇakusalau, samādhissa gaucarakusalau, samādhissa  abhinīhārakusalau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sāriputtau cittaṃ vasai vattaiti, nau ca sāriputtau cittassa vasaina vattatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. paṭhamaniddasasuttaṃ

42. atha  khau āyasmā sāriputtau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| atha khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa  aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| taina khau pana samayaina taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘yau hi kauci, āvusau, dvādasavassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddasau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandi nappaṭik‍kausi| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi – ‘‘bhagavatau santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ  nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘atippagau khau tāva sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’nti| atha khvāhaṃ, bhantai, yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudiṃ| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ| taina khau pana, bhantai, samayaina taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ  ayamantarākathā udapādi  – ‘yau hi kauci, āvusau, dvādasavassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, niddasau bhikkhūti alaṃ vacanāyā’ti| atha khvāhaṃ, bhantai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandiṃ nappaṭik‍kausiṃ| anabhinanditvā  appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃ pak‍kāmiṃ (sī॰ syā॰) – ‘bhagavatau santikai aitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmī’ti| sak‍kā nu khau, bhantai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai kaivalaṃ vassagaṇanamattaina niddasau bhikkhu pañ‍ñāpaitu’’nti?

‘‘na khau, sāriputta, sak‍kā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai kaivalaṃ vassagaṇanamattaina niddasau bhikkhu pañ‍ñāpaituṃ| satta khau imāni, sāriputta, niddasavatthūni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni|

a॰ ni॰ 7.20; dī॰ ni॰ 3.331 ‘‘katamāni satta? idha, sāriputta, bhikkhu sikkhāsamādānai tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca sikkhāsamādānai avigatapaimau, dhammanisantiyā tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca dhammanisantiyā  avigatapaimau, icchāvinayai tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca icchāvinayai avigatapaimau, paṭisal‍lānai tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca paṭisal‍lānai avigatapaimau, vīriyārambhai tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca vīriyārambhai avigatapaimau, satinaipak‍kai tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca satinaipak‍kai avigatapaimau, diṭṭhipaṭivaidhai tibbacchandau hauti āyatiñ‍ca diṭṭhipaṭivaidhai avigatapaimau| imāni khau, sāriputta, satta niddasavatthūni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni| imaihi khau, sāriputta, sattahi niddasavatthūhi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu dvādasa caipi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddasau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya; catubbīsati caipi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddasau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ  vacanāya; chattiṃsati caipi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddasau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya, aṭṭhacattārīsaṃ caipi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddasau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. dutiyaniddasasuttaṃ

43. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya  kausambiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva kausambiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha  khau āyasmā ānandau yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

taina khau pana samayaina taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘yau hi kauci, āvusau, dvādasa vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddasau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandi nappaṭik‍kausi| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi – ‘‘bhagavatau santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau kausambiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya kausambiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘atippagau khau tāva kausambiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’nti…pai॰… taihi saddhiṃ sammaudiṃ| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ|

‘‘taina khau pana, bhantai, samayaina taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ  san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘yau hi kauci, āvusau, dvādasavassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, niddasau bhikkhūti alaṃ vacanāyā’ti| atha khvāhaṃ, bhantai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandiṃ nappaṭik‍kausiṃ| anabhinanditvā, appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃ – ‘bhagavatau santikai aitassa bhāsitassa  atthaṃ ājānissāmī’ti| sak‍kā nu khau, bhantai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai kaivalaṃ vassagaṇanamattaina niddasau bhikkhu pañ‍ñāpaitu’’nti?

‘‘na  khau, ānanda, sak‍kā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai kaivalaṃ vassagaṇanamattaina niddasau bhikkhu pañ‍ñāpaituṃ| satta khau imāni, ānanda, niddasavatthūni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni|

‘‘katamāni satta? idhānanda, bhikkhu, saddhau hauti, hirīmā hauti, auttappī hauti, bahussutau hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, satimā hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti| imāni khau, ānanda, satta niddasavatthūni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni| imaihi  khau, ānanda, sattahi niddasavatthūhi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu dvādasa caipi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddasau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya; catubbīsati caipi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddasau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya; chattiṃsati caipi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddasau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya, aṭṭhacattārīsaṃ caipi vassāni paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, ‘niddasau  bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

daivatāvaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

appamādau hirī caiva, dvai suvacā duvai mittā|

dvai paṭisambhidā dvai vasā, duvai niddasavatthunāti||


5. mahāyañ‍ñavaggau

1. sattaviñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitisuttaṃ

44. dī॰ ni॰ 3.332; cūḷani॰ pausālamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 83 ‘‘sattimā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau| katamā satta? santi, bhikkhavai, sattā nānattakāyā nānattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi manussā, aikac‍cai ca daivā, aikac‍cai ca vinipātikā| ayaṃ paṭhamā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā nānattakāyā aikattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā brahmakāyikā paṭhamābhinibbattā| ayaṃ dutiyā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā aikattakāyā nānattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā ābhassarā| ayaṃ tatiyā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā aikattakāyā aikattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā subhakiṇhā| ayaṃ catutthā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ pañ‍camā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ chaṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ sattamā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti| imā khau, bhikkhavai, satta viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. samādhiparikkhārasuttaṃ

45. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, samādhiparikkhārā| katamai satta? sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati| yā khau, bhikkhavai, imaihi sattahaṅgaihi cittassaikaggatā parikkhatā ‘saparikkhāratā’tipi, ‘saparikkhatā’tipi (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.28), ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyau sammāsamādhi samādhi (syā॰) saupanisau itipi saparikkhārau itipī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamaaggisuttaṃ

46. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, aggī| katamai satta? rāgaggi, dausaggi, mauhaggi, āhunaiyyaggi, gahapataggi, dakkhiṇaiyyaggi, kaṭṭhaggi – imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta aggī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyaaggisuttaṃ

47. taina khau pana samayaina uggatasarīrassa brāhmaṇassa mahāyañ‍ñau upakkhaṭau hauti| pañ‍ca usabhasatāni thūṇūpanītāni haunti yañ‍ñatthāya, pañ‍ca vacchatarasatāni thūṇūpanītāni haunti yañ‍ñatthāya, pañ‍ca vacchatarisatāni thūṇūpanītāni haunti yañ‍ñatthāya, pañ‍ca ajasatāni thūṇūpanītāni haunti yañ‍ñatthāya, pañ‍ca urabbhasatāni thūṇūpanītāni haunti yañ‍ñatthāya| atha khau uggatasarīrau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā  saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uggatasarīrau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau gautama, aggissa ādānaṃ ādhānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) yūpassa ussāpanaṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahānisaṃsa’’nti| ‘‘mayāpi khau aitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sutaṃ aggissa ādānaṃ yūpassa ussāpanaṃ mahapphalaṃ  hauti mahānisaṃsa’’nti| dutiyampi khau uggatasarīrau brāhmaṇau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau uggatasarīrau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau gautama, aggissa ādānaṃ yūpassa ussāpanaṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahānisaṃsa’’nti| ‘‘mayāpi khau aitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sutaṃ aggissa ādānaṃ yūpassa ussāpanaṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahānisaṃsa’’nti| ‘‘tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, samaiti bhautau caiva gautamassa amhākañ‍ca, yadidaṃ sabbaina sabbaṃ’’|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā ānandau uggatasarīraṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatā aivaṃ pucchitabbā – ‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau gautama, aggissa ādānaṃ yūpassa ussāpanaṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahānisaṃsa’nti| aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatā pucchitabbā – ‘ahañhi, bhantai, aggiṃ aggissa (ka॰) ādātukāmau, ādhātukāmau (sī॰ syā॰) yūpaṃ ussāpaitukāmau| auvadatu maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā| anusāsatu maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’’ti|

atha khau uggatasarīrau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahañhi, bhau gautama, aggiṃ ādātukāmau yūpaṃ ussāpaitukāmau| auvadatu maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau| anusāsatu maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘aggiṃ, brāhmaṇa, ādaintau ādhaintau (sī॰ syā॰) yūpaṃ  ussāpaintau pubbaiva yañ‍ñā tīṇi satthāni ussāpaiti akusalāni dukkhudrayāni dukkhuddayāni (sī॰) dukkhavipākāni| katamāni tīṇi? kāyasatthaṃ, vacīsatthaṃ, manausatthaṃ| aggiṃ, brāhmaṇa, ādaintau yūpaṃ ussāpaintau pubbaiva yañ‍ñā aivaṃ cittaṃ uppādaisi – ‘aittakā usabhā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā vacchatarā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā vacchatariyau hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā ajā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā urabbhā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāyā’ti| sau ‘puñ‍ñaṃ karaumī’ti apuñ‍ñaṃ karauti, ‘kusalaṃ karaumī’ti akusalaṃ karauti, ‘sugatiyā maggaṃ pariyaisāmī’ti duggatiyā maggaṃ pariyaisati| aggiṃ, brāhmaṇa, ādaintau yūpaṃ ussāpaintau  pubbaiva yañ‍ñā idaṃ paṭhamaṃ manausatthaṃ ussāpaiti akusalaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipākaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aggiṃ ādaintau yūpaṃ ussāpaintau pubbaiva yañ‍ñā aivaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati – ‘aittakā usabhā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā  vacchatarā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā vacchatariyau hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā ajā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā urabbhā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāyā’ti| sau ‘puñ‍ñaṃ karaumī’ti apuñ‍ñaṃ karauti, ‘kusalaṃ karaumī’ti akusalaṃ karauti, ‘sugatiyā maggaṃ pariyaisāmī’ti duggatiyā maggaṃ pariyaisati| aggiṃ, brāhmaṇa, ādaintau yūpaṃ ussāpaintau pubbaiva yañ‍ñā idaṃ dutiyaṃ vacīsatthaṃ ussāpaiti akusalaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipākaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aggiṃ ādaintau yūpaṃ ussāpaintau pubbaiva yañ‍ñā sayaṃ paṭhamaṃ samārambhati samārabbhati (sī॰ ka॰), samārabhati (syā॰) usabhā  hantuṃ hañ‍ñantu (sī॰ syā॰) yañ‍ñatthāya, sayaṃ paṭhamaṃ samārambhati vacchatarā hantuṃ yañ‍ñatthāya, sayaṃ paṭhamaṃ samārambhati vacchatariyau hantuṃ yañ‍ñatthāya, sayaṃ paṭhamaṃ samārambhati ajā hantuṃ yañ‍ñatthāya, sayaṃ paṭhamaṃ samārambhati urabbhā hantuṃ yañ‍ñatthāya hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāyāti (sī॰ syā॰)| sau ‘puñ‍ñaṃ karaumī’ti apuñ‍ñaṃ karauti, ‘kusalaṃ karaumī’ti akusalaṃ karauti, ‘sugatiyā maggaṃ pariyaisāmī’ti duggatiyā maggaṃ pariyaisati| aggiṃ, brāhmaṇa, ādaintau yūpaṃ ussāpaintau pubbaiva yañ‍ñā idaṃ tatiyaṃ kāyasatthaṃ ussāpaiti akusalaṃ dukkhudrayaṃ dukkhavipākaṃ| aggiṃ, brāhmaṇa, ādaintau yūpaṃ ussāpaintau pubbaiva yañ‍ñā imāni tīṇi satthāni ussāpaiti akusalāni dukkhudrayāni dukkhavipākāni|

‘‘tayaumai, brāhmaṇa, aggī pahātabbā parivaj‍jaitabbā, na saivitabbā| katamai tayau? rāgaggi, dausaggi, mauhaggi|

‘‘kasmā cāyaṃ, brāhmaṇa, rāgaggi pahātabbau parivaj‍jaitabbau, na saivitabbau? rattau khau, brāhmaṇa, rāgaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| tasmāyaṃ rāgaggi pahātabbau parivaj‍jaitabbau, na saivitabbau|

‘‘kasmā cāyaṃ, brāhmaṇa, dausaggi pahātabbau parivaj‍jaitabbau, na saivitabbau? duṭṭhau khau, brāhmaṇa, dausaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ  carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, manasā  duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| tasmāyaṃ dausaggi pahātabbau parivaj‍jaitabbau, na saivitabbau|

‘‘kasmā cāyaṃ, brāhmaṇa, mauhaggi pahātabbau parivaj‍jaitabbau, na saivitabbau? mūḷhau khau, brāhmaṇa, mauhaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| tasmāyaṃ mauhaggi pahātabbau parivaj‍jaitabbau, na saivitabbau| imai khau tayau, brāhmaṇa, aggī pahātabbā parivaj‍jaitabbā, na saivitabbā|

‘‘tayau khau, brāhmaṇa, aggī sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā sammā sukhaṃ parihātabbā| katamai tayau? āhunaiyyaggi, gahapataggi, dakkhiṇaiyyaggi|

‘‘katamau ca, brāhmaṇa, āhunaiyyaggi? idha, brāhmaṇa, yassa tai haunti mātāti vā pitāti vā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, āhunaiyyaggi| taṃ kissa haitu? atauhayaṃ itaupāyaṃ (ka॰), brāhmaṇa, āhutau sambhūtau, tasmāyaṃ āhunaiyyaggi sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā sammā sukhaṃ parihātabbau|

‘‘katamau ca, brāhmaṇa, gahapataggi? idha, brāhmaṇa, yassa tai haunti puttāti vā dārāti vā dāsāti vā paissāti vā kammakarāti vā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, gahapataggi| tasmāyaṃ gahapataggi sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā sammā sukhaṃ parihātabbau|

‘‘katamau ca, brāhmaṇa, dakkhiṇaiyyaggi? idha, brāhmaṇa, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā parappavādā paṭiviratā khantisaurac‍cai niviṭṭhā aikamattānaṃ damainti, aikamattānaṃ samainti, aikamattānaṃ parinibbāpainti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, dakkhiṇaiyyaggi| tasmāyaṃ dakkhiṇaiyyaggi sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā sammā sukhaṃ parihātabbau| imai khau, brāhmaṇa, tayau aggī sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā sammā sukhaṃ parihātabbā|

‘‘ayaṃ  khau pana, brāhmaṇa, kaṭṭhaggi kālaina kālaṃ uj‍jalaitabbau, kālaina kālaṃ ajjhupaikkhitabbau, kālaina kālaṃ nibbāpaitabbau, kālaina kālaṃ nikkhipitabbau’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai uggatasarīrau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama; abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ  saraṇaṃ gatanti| aisāhaṃ, bhau gautama, pañ‍ca usabhasatāni muñ‍cāmi jīvitaṃ daimi, pañ‍ca vacchatarasatāni muñ‍cāmi jīvitaṃ daimi, pañ‍ca vacchatarisatāni muñ‍cāmi jīvitaṃ daimi, pañ‍ca ajasatāni muñ‍cāmi jīvitaṃ daimi, pañ‍ca urabbhasatāni muñ‍cāmi jīvitaṃ daimi| haritāni caiva tiṇāni khādantu, sītāni ca pānīyāni pivantu, sītau ca naisaṃ vātau upavāyata’’nti upavāyatūti (ka॰)| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamasañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

48. ‘‘sattimā, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā|

‘‘katamā  satta? asubhasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā, anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, satta sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyasañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

49. ‘‘sattimā, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā| katamā satta? asubhasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā, anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, satta sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānāti|

‘‘‘asubhasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti| iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? asubhasañ‍ñāparicitaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau maithunadhammasamāpattiyā cittaṃ patilīyati  patikuṭati pativattati, na sampasāriyati upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭapattaṃ vā nhārudaddulaṃ vā aggimhi pakkhittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati, na sampasāriyati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau asubhasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau maithunadhammasamāpattiyā cittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati, na sampasāriyati upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau asubhasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau maithunadhammasamāpattiyā cittaṃ anusandahati anusaṇṭhāti (sī॰) appaṭikulyatā  saṇṭhāti; vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘abhāvitā mai asubhasañ‍ñā, natthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, appattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| sacai pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau asubhasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau maithunadhammasamāpattiyā cittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati, na sampasāriyati upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti; vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘subhāvitā mai asubhasañ‍ñā, atthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, pattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| ‘asubhasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘maraṇasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā  hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? maraṇasañ‍ñāparicitaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau jīvitanikantiyā cittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati, na sampasāriyati upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭapattaṃ vā nhārudaddulaṃ vā aggimhi pakkhittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati, na sampasāriyati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau maraṇasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau jīvitanikantiyā cittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati, na sampasāriyati upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau maraṇasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau jīvitanikantiyā  cittaṃ anusandahati appaṭikulyatā saṇṭhāti; vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘abhāvitā mai maraṇasañ‍ñā, natthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, appattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| sacai pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau maraṇasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau jīvitanikantiyā cittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati, na sampasāriyati upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti; vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘subhāvitā mai maraṇasañ‍ñā, atthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, pattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| ‘maraṇasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti  mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñāparicitaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau rasataṇhāya cittaṃ patilīyati  …pai॰… upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭapattaṃ vā nhārudaddulaṃ vā aggimhi pakkhittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati, na sampasāriyati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau rasataṇhāya cittaṃ patilīyati…pai॰… upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau rasataṇhāya cittaṃ anusandahati appaṭikulyatā saṇṭhāti; vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘abhāvitā mai āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, natthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, appattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| sacai pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau rasataṇhāya cittaṃ patilīyati…pai॰… upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti; vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘subhāvitā mai āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, atthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, pattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau  hauti| ‘āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñāparicitaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau laukacitraisu cittaṃ patilīyati…pai॰… saiyyathāpi bhikkhavai…pai॰… patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati, na sampasāriyati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau laukacitraisu  cittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati pativattati, na sampasāriyati upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau laukacitraisu cittaṃ anusandahati appaṭikulyatā saṇṭhāti; vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘abhāvitā mai sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā, natthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, appattaṃ mai  bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| sacai pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau  sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau laukacitraisu cittaṃ patilīyati…pai॰… upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti; vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘subhāvitā mai sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā, atthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, pattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| ‘sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘anic‍casañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? anic‍casañ‍ñāparicitaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau lābhasak‍kārasilaukai cittaṃ patilīyati…pai॰… upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭapattaṃ vā nhārudaddulaṃ vā aggimhi pakkhittaṃ patilīyati  patikuṭati pativattati na sampasāriyati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau anic‍casañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau  lābhasak‍kārasilaukai cittaṃ patilīyati…pai॰… upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau anic‍casañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau lābhasak‍kārasilaukai cittaṃ anusandahati appaṭikulyatā saṇṭhāti; vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘abhāvitā mai anic‍casañ‍ñā, natthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, appattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| sacai pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau anic‍casañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau lābhasak‍kārasilaukai cittaṃ patilīyati patikuṭati  pativattati, na sampasāriyati upaikkhā vā pāṭikulyatā vā saṇṭhāti; vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘subhāvitā mai anic‍casañ‍ñā, atthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, pattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| ‘anic‍casañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘anic‍cai  dukkhasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñāparicitaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau ālasyai kausaj‍jai vissaṭṭhiyai pamādai ananuyaugai apac‍cavaikkhaṇāya tibbā bhayasañ‍ñā pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti, saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ukkhittāsikai  vadhakai|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau ālasyai kausaj‍jai vissaṭṭhiyai pamādai ananuyaugai apac‍cavaikkhaṇāya tibbā bhayasañ‍ñā, na pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti, saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ukkhittāsikai vadhakai| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘abhāvitā mai anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, natthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, appattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| sacai pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau ālasyai kausaj‍jai vissaṭṭhiyai pamādai ananuyaugai apac‍cavaikkhaṇāya tibbā bhayasañ‍ñā pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti, saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ukkhittāsikai vadhakai| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘subhāvitā mai anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, atthi mai pubbaināparaṃ  visaisau, pattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| ‘anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti  mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? dukkhai anattasañ‍ñāparicitaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānāpagataṃ mānasaṃ hauti vidhāsamatik‍kantaṃ santaṃ suvimuttaṃ|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau dukkhai anattasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau imasmiñ‍ca  saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu na ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānāpagataṃ mānasaṃ hauti vidhāsamatik‍kantaṃ santaṃ suvimuttaṃ| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘abhāvitā mai dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, natthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, appattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti|

‘‘sacai pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau dukkhai anattasañ‍ñāparicitaina caitasā bahulaṃ viharatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānāpagataṃ mānasaṃ hauti vidhāsamatik‍kantaṃ santaṃ suvimuttaṃ| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘subhāvitā mai dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, atthi mai pubbaināparaṃ visaisau, pattaṃ mai bhāvanābala’nti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| ‘dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘imā khau, bhikkhavai, satta sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. maithunasuttaṃ

50. atha  khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhavampi nau gautamau brahmacārī paṭijānātī’’ti? ‘‘yañhi taṃ, brāhmaṇa, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘akhaṇḍaṃ acchiddaṃ asabalaṃ akammāsaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caratī’ti, mamaiva  taṃ, brāhmaṇa, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, akhaṇḍaṃ acchiddaṃ asabalaṃ akammāsaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carāmī’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, brahmacariyassa khaṇḍampi chiddampi sabalampi kammāsampī’’ti?

‘‘idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā sammā brahmacārī paṭijānamānau na haiva khau mātugāmaina saddhiṃ dvayaṃdvayasamāpattiṃ samāpaj‍jati; api ca khau mātugāmassa ucchādanaparimaddananhāpanasambāhanaṃ sādiyati| sau taṃ assādaiti sau tadassādaiti (sī॰), taṃ nikāmaiti, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| idampi khau, brāhmaṇa, brahmacariyassa khaṇḍampi chiddampi sabalampi kammāsampi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, aparisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati, saṃyuttau maithunaina saṃyaugaina na parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina jarāmaraṇaina (sī॰ syā॰) saukaihi  paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, na parimuc‍cati dukkhasmāti vadāmi|
