‘‘puna  caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, idhaikac‍cau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā sammā brahmacārī paṭijānamānau na haiva khau mātugāmaina saddhiṃ dvayaṃdvayasamāpattiṃ samāpaj‍jati, napi mātugāmassa ucchādanaparimaddananhāpanasambāhanaṃ sādiyati; api ca khau mātugāmaina saddhiṃ sañ‍jagghati saṃkīḷati saṃkailāyati…pai॰… napi mātugāmaina saddhiṃ sañ‍jagghati saṃkīḷati saṃkailāyati; api ca khau mātugāmassa cakkhunā cakkhuṃ upanijjhāyati paikkhati…pai॰… napi mātugāmassa cakkhunā cakkhuṃ upanijjhāyati paikkhati; api ca khau mātugāmassa saddaṃ suṇāti  tiraukuṭṭaṃ vā tiraupākāraṃ vā hasantiyā vā bhaṇantiyā vā gāyantiyā vā raudantiyā vā…pai॰… napi mātugāmassa saddaṃ suṇāti tiraukuṭṭaṃ vā tiraupākāraṃ vā hasantiyā vā bhaṇantiyā vā gāyantiyā vā raudantiyā vā; api ca khau yānissa tāni pubbai  mātugāmaina saddhiṃ hasitalapitakīḷitāni tāni anussarati…pai॰… napi yānissa tāni pubbai mātugāmaina saddhiṃ hasitalapitakīḷitāni tāni anussarati; api ca khau passati gahapatiṃ vā gahapatiputtaṃ vā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitaṃ samaṅgībhūtaṃ paricārayamānaṃ…pai॰… napi passati gahapatiṃ vā gahapatiputtaṃ vā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitaṃ samaṅgībhūtaṃ paricārayamānaṃ; api ca khau añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vāti| sau taṃ assādaiti, taṃ nikāmaiti, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| idampi khau, brāhmaṇa, brahmacariyassa khaṇḍampi chiddampi sabalampi kammāsampi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, aparisuddhaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ carati saṃyuttau maithunaina saṃyaugaina, na parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, na parimuc‍cati dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ maithunasaṃyaugānaṃ añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataramaithunasaṃyaugaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ maithunasaṃyaugaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) attani appahīnaṃ samanupassiṃ, naiva tāvāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ  sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ abhisambuddhau pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

‘‘yatau  ca khauhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ maithunasaṃyaugānaṃ añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataramaithunasaṃyaugaṃ attani appahīnaṃ na samanupassiṃ, athāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ‘ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi, akuppā mai vimutti caitauvimutti (sī॰ ka॰), ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama; abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. saṃyaugasuttaṃ

51. ‘‘saṃyaugavisaṃyaugaṃ  vau, bhikkhavai, dhammapariyāyaṃ daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamau ca sau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaugau visaṃyaugau dhammapariyāyau?

‘‘itthī, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ itthindriyaṃ manasi karauti – itthikuttaṃ itthākappaṃ itthividhaṃ itthicchandaṃ itthissaraṃ itthālaṅkāraṃ| sā tattha raj‍jati tatrābhiramati| sā tattha rattā tatrābhiratā bahiddhā purisindriyaṃ manasi karauti – purisakuttaṃ purisākappaṃ purisavidhaṃ purisacchandaṃ purisassaraṃ purisālaṅkāraṃ| sā tattha raj‍jati tatrābhiramati| sā tattha rattā tatrābhiratā bahiddhā saṃyaugaṃ ākaṅkhati| yañ‍cassā saṃyaugapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ  tañ‍ca ākaṅkhati| itthattai, bhikkhavai, abhiratā sattā purisaisu saṃyaugaṃ gatā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, itthī itthattaṃ nātivattati|

‘‘purisau, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ purisindriyaṃ manasi karauti – purisakuttaṃ purisākappaṃ purisavidhaṃ purisacchandaṃ purisassaraṃ purisālaṅkāraṃ| sau tattha raj‍jati tatrābhiramati| sau tattha rattau tatrābhiratau bahiddhā itthindriyaṃ manasi karauti – itthikuttaṃ itthākappaṃ itthividhaṃ itthicchandaṃ itthissaraṃ itthālaṅkāraṃ| sau tattha raj‍jati tatrābhiramati| sau tattha rattau tatrābhiratau bahiddhā saṃyaugaṃ ākaṅkhati| yañ‍cassa saṃyaugapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ tañ‍ca ākaṅkhati| purisattai, bhikkhavai, abhiratā sattā  itthīsu saṃyaugaṃ gatā| aivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, purisau purisattaṃ nātivattati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaugau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, visaṃyaugau hauti? itthī, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ itthindriyaṃ na manasi karauti – itthikuttaṃ itthākappaṃ itthividhaṃ itthicchandaṃ itthissaraṃ itthālaṅkāraṃ| sā tattha na raj‍jati, sā tatra nābhiramati| sā tattha arattā tatra anabhiratā bahiddhā purisindriyaṃ na manasi karauti – purisakuttaṃ purisākappaṃ purisavidhaṃ purisacchandaṃ purisassaraṃ purisālaṅkāraṃ| sā tattha na raj‍jati, tatra nābhiramati| sā tattha arattā tatra anabhiratā bahiddhā saṃyaugaṃ nākaṅkhati| yañ‍cassā saṃyaugapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ tañ‍ca nākaṅkhati| itthattai, bhikkhavai, anabhiratā sattā purisaisu visaṃyaugaṃ gatā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, itthī itthattaṃ  ativattati|

‘‘purisau, bhikkhavai, ajjhattaṃ purisindriyaṃ na manasi karauti – purisakuttaṃ purisākappaṃ purisavidhaṃ purisacchandaṃ purisassaraṃ purisālaṅkāraṃ| sau tattha na raj‍jati, sau tatra nābhiramati| sau tattha arattau tatra anabhiratau bahiddhā itthindriyaṃ na manasi karauti – itthikuttaṃ itthākappaṃ itthividhaṃ itthicchandaṃ itthissaraṃ itthālaṅkāraṃ| sau tattha na raj‍jati, tatra nābhiramati| sau tattha arattau tatra anabhiratau bahiddhā saṃyaugaṃ nākaṅkhati| yañ‍cassa saṃyaugapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ tañ‍ca nākaṅkhati| purisattai, bhikkhavai, anabhiratā sattā itthīsu visaṃyaugaṃ gatā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, purisau purisattaṃ ativattati| aivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, visaṃyaugau hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaugau visaṃyaugau dhammapariyāyau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dānamahapphalasuttaṃ

52. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai| atha khau sambahulā campaiyyakā upāsakā yaina āyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau campaiyyakā upāsakā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘cirassutā nau, bhantai bhantai sāriputta (sī॰), bhagavatau  sammukhā dhammīkathā| sādhu mayaṃ, bhantai, labhaiyyāma bhagavatau sammukhā dhammiṃ kathaṃ bhagavatau santikā dhammiṃ kathaṃ (sī॰), bhagavatau dhammiṃ kathaṃ (syā॰) savanāyā’’ti| ‘‘tainahāvusau, tadahupausathai āgacchaiyyātha, appaiva nāma labhaiyyātha bhagavatau sammukhā bhagavatau santikai (syā॰) dhammiṃ kathaṃ savanāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau campaiyyakā upāsakā āyasmatau sāriputtassa paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā  āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kamiṃsu|

atha khau campaiyyakā upāsakā tadahupausathai yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau taihi campaiyyakaihi upāsakaihi saddhiṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘siyā  nu khau, bhantai, idhaikac‍cassa tādisaṃyaiva dānaṃ din‍naṃ na mahapphalaṃ hauti na mahānisaṃsaṃ; siyā pana, bhantai, idhaikac‍cassa tādisaṃyaiva dānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahānisaṃsa’’nti? ‘‘siyā, sāriputta, idhaikac‍cassa tādisaṃyaiva dānaṃ din‍naṃ na mahapphalaṃ hauti na mahānisaṃsaṃ; siyā pana, sāriputta, idhaikac‍cassa tādisaṃyaiva dānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahānisaṃsa’’nti| ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cassa tādisaṃyaiva dānaṃ din‍naṃ na mahapphalaṃ hauti na mahānisaṃsaṃ; kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cassa tādisaṃyaiva dānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahānisaṃsa’’nti?

‘‘idha, sāriputta, aikac‍cau sāpaikhau sāpaikkhau (syā॰) dānaṃ daiti, patibaddhacittau patibandhacittau (ka॰) dānaṃ daiti, san‍nidhipaikhau dānaṃ daiti, ‘imaṃ paic‍ca paribhuñ‍jissāmī’ti dānaṃ daiti| sau taṃ dānaṃ daiti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sāriputta, dadaiyya idhaikac‍cau aivarūpaṃ dāna’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tatra, sāriputta, yvāyaṃ sāpaikhau dānaṃ daiti, patibaddhacittau dānaṃ daiti, san‍nidhipaikhau dānaṃ daiti, ‘imaṃ paic‍ca paribhuñ‍jissāmī’ti dānaṃ daiti| sau  taṃ dānaṃ datvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| sau taṃ kammaṃ  khaipaitvā taṃ iddhiṃ taṃ yasaṃ taṃ ādhipac‍caṃ āgāmī hauti āgantā itthattaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, sāriputta, aikac‍cau na haiva khau sāpaikhau dānaṃ daiti, na patibaddhacittau dānaṃ daiti, na san‍nidhipaikhau dānaṃ daiti, na ‘imaṃ paic‍ca paribhuñ‍jissāmī’ti dānaṃ daiti; api ca khau ‘sāhu dāna’nti dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… napi ‘sāhu dāna’nti dānaṃ daiti; api ca khau ‘din‍napubbaṃ katapubbaṃ pitupitāmahaihi na arahāmi paurāṇaṃ kulavaṃsaṃ hāpaitu’nti dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… napi ‘din‍napubbaṃ katapubbaṃ pitupitāmahaihi na arahāmi paurāṇaṃ kulavaṃsaṃ hāpaitu’nti dānaṃ daiti; api ca khau ‘ahaṃ pacāmi, imai na pacanti, nārahāmi pacantau apacantānaṃ dānaṃ adātu’nti dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… napi ‘ahaṃ pacāmi, imai na pacanti, nārahāmi pacantau apacantānaṃ dānaṃ adātu’nti dānaṃ daiti; api ca khau ‘yathā taisaṃ pubbakānaṃ isīnaṃ tāni mahāyañ‍ñāni ahaisuṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – aṭṭhakassa vāmakassa vāmadaivassa vaissāmittassa yamadagginau aṅgīrasassa bhāradvājassa vāsaiṭṭhassa kassapassa bhagunau, aivaṃ mai ayaṃ  dānasaṃvibhāgau bhavissatī’ti dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… napi ‘yathā taisaṃ pubbakānaṃ isīnaṃ tāni mahāyañ‍ñāni ahaisuṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – aṭṭhakassa vāmakassa vāmadaivassa vaissāmittassa yamadagginau aṅgīrasassa bhāradvājassa vāsaiṭṭhassa  kassapassa bhagunau, aivaṃ mai ayaṃ dānasaṃvibhāgau  bhavissatī’ti dānaṃ daiti; api ca khau ‘imaṃ mai dānaṃ dadatau cittaṃ pasīdati, attamanatā saumanassaṃ upajāyatī’ti dānaṃ daiti…pai॰… napi ‘imaṃ mai dānaṃ dadatau cittaṃ pasīdati, attamanatā saumanassaṃ upajāyatī’ti dānaṃ daiti; api ca khau cittālaṅkāracittaparikkhāraṃ dānaṃ daiti| sau taṃ dānaṃ daiti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sāriputta, dadaiyya idhaikac‍cau aivarūpaṃ dāna’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tatra, sāriputta, yvāyaṃ na haiva nahaiva khau (sī॰ syā॰) sāpaikhau dānaṃ daiti; na patibaddhacittau dānaṃ daiti; na san‍nidhipaikhau dānaṃ daiti; na ‘imaṃ paic‍ca paribhuñ‍jissāmī’ti dānaṃ daiti; napi ‘sāhu dāna’nti dānaṃ daiti; napi ‘din‍napubbaṃ katapubbaṃ pitupitāmahaihi  na arahāmi paurāṇaṃ kulavaṃsaṃ hāpaitu’nti dānaṃ daiti; napi ‘ahaṃ pacāmi, imai na pacanti, nārahāmi pacantau apacantānaṃ dānaṃ adātu’nti dānaṃ daiti; napi ‘yathā taisaṃ pubbakānaṃ isīnaṃ tāni mahāyañ‍ñāni ahaisuṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – aṭṭhakassa vāmakassa vāmadaivassa vaissāmittassa yamadagginau aṅgīrasassa bhāradvājassa vāsaiṭṭhassa kassapassa bhagunau, aivaṃ mai ayaṃ dānasaṃvibhāgau bhavissatī’ti dānaṃ daiti; napi ‘imaṃ mai dānaṃ dadatau cittaṃ pasīdati, attamanatā saumanassaṃ upajāyatī’ti dānaṃ daiti; api ca khau cittālaṅkāracittaparikkhāraṃ dānaṃ daiti| sau taṃ dānaṃ datvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā brahmakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ  upapaj‍jati| sau taṃ kammaṃ  khaipaitvā taṃ iddhiṃ taṃ yasaṃ taṃ ādhipac‍caṃ anāgāmī hauti anāgantā itthattaṃ| ayaṃ khau, sāriputta, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cassa tādisaṃyaiva dānaṃ din‍naṃ na mahapphalaṃ hauti na mahānisaṃsaṃ| ayaṃ pana, sāriputta, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cassa tādisaṃyaiva dānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahānisaṃsa’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. nandamātāsuttaṃ

53. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau  dakkhiṇāgirismiṃ cārikaṃ caranti mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ| taina khau pana samayaina vaiḷukaṇḍakī vaiḷukaṇḍakī (syā॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.37; 2.134; saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.173 passitabbaṃ nandamātā upāsikā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya pārāyanaṃ cūḷani॰ pārāyanavagga, vatthugāthā saraina bhāsati|

taina khau pana samayaina vaissavaṇau mahārājā uttarāya disāya dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ gacchati kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| assausi khau vaissavaṇau mahārājā nandamātāya upāsikāya pārāyanaṃ saraina bhāsantiyā, sutvā kathāpariyausānaṃ āgamayamānau aṭṭhāsi|

atha khau nandamātā upāsikā pārāyanaṃ saraina bhāsitvā tuṇhī ahausi| atha khau vaissavaṇau mahārājā nandamātāya upāsikāya kathāpariyausānaṃ viditvā abbhānumaudi – ‘‘sādhu bhagini, sādhu bhaginī’’ti! ‘‘kau panaisau, bhadramukhā’’ti? ‘‘ahaṃ tai, bhagini, bhātā vaissavaṇau, mahārājā’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, bhadramukha, taina hi yau mai ayaṃ dhammapariyāyau bhaṇitau  idaṃ tai hautu ātithaiyya’’nti| ‘‘sādhu, bhagini, aitañ‍caiva mai hautu  ātithaiyyaṃ| svaiva svai ca (sī॰) sāriputtamauggal‍lānappamukhau  bhikkhusaṅghau akatapātarāsau vaiḷukaṇḍakaṃ āgamissati, tañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghaṃ parivisitvā mama dakkhiṇaṃ ādisaiyyāsi| aitañ‍caiva aivañ‍ca (sī॰ syā॰), aitañ‍ca (?) mai bhavissati ātithaiyya’’nti|

atha khau nandamātā upāsikā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina sakai nivaisanai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaisi| atha khau sāriputtamauggal‍lānappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau akatapātarāsau yaina vaiḷukaṇḍakau tadavasari| atha khau nandamātā upāsikā añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhusaṅghassa kālaṃ āraucaihi – ‘kālau, bhantai, ayyāya nandamātuyā nivaisanai niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ayyai’’ti khau sau purisau nandamātāya upāsikāya paṭissutvā ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhusaṅghassa kālaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, ayyāya nandamātuyā nivaisanai niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau sāriputtamauggal‍lānappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina nandamātāya upāsikāya nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau nandamātā upāsikā sāriputtamauggal‍lānappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi|

atha  khau nandamātā upāsikā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau nandamātaraṃ upāsikaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca – ‘‘kau pana tai, nandamātai, bhikkhusaṅghassa abbhāgamanaṃ āraucaisī’’ti?

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya pārāyanaṃ saraina bhāsitvā tuṇhī ahausiṃ| atha khau, bhantai, vaissavaṇau mahārājā  mama kathāpariyausānaṃ viditvā abbhānumaudi – ‘sādhu, bhagini, sādhu, bhaginī’ti! ‘kau panaisau, bhadramukhā’ti? ‘ahaṃ tai, bhagini, bhātā vaissavaṇau, mahārājā’ti| ‘sādhu, bhadramukha, taina hi yau mai ayaṃ dhammapariyāyau  bhaṇitau idaṃ tai hautu ātithaiyya’nti| ‘sādhu, bhagini, aitañ‍caiva mai hautu ātithaiyyaṃ| svaiva sāriputtamauggal‍lānappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau akatapātarāsau vaiḷukaṇḍakaṃ āgamissati, tañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghaṃ parivisitvā mama dakkhiṇaṃ ādisaiyyāsi| aitañ‍caiva aitañ‍ca (sī॰), aivañ‍ca (syā॰) mai bhavissati ātithaiyya’nti| yadidaṃ yamidaṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 1.363), bhantai, dānai puñ‍ñaṃ hi taṃ (sī॰), puñ‍ñaṃ puñ‍ñamahitaṃ (syā॰), puñ‍ñaṃ vā puñ‍ñamahaṃ vā (pī॰), puñ‍ñaṃ vā puñ‍ñamahī vā (ka॰) puñ‍ñañ‍ca puñ‍ñamahī ca taṃ puñ‍ñaṃ hi taṃ (sī॰), puñ‍ñaṃ puñ‍ñamahitaṃ (syā॰), puñ‍ñaṃ vā puñ‍ñamahaṃ vā (pī॰), puñ‍ñaṃ vā puñ‍ñamahī vā (ka॰) vaissavaṇassa mahārājassa sukhāya hautū’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, nandamātai, abbhutaṃ, nandamātai! yatra hi nāma vaissavaṇaina mahārājaina aivaṃmahiddhikaina aivaṃmahaisakkhaina daivaputtaina sammukhā sal‍lapissasī’’ti|

‘‘na khau mai, bhantai, aisaiva acchariyau abbhutau dhammau| atthi mai añ‍ñaupi acchariyau abbhutau dhammau| idha mai, bhantai, nandau nāma aikaputtakau piyau manāpau| taṃ rājānau kismiñ‍cidaiva pakaraṇai aukassa pasayha jīvitā vauraupaisuṃ| tasmiṃ khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, dārakai gahitai vā gayhamānai vā vadhai vā vajjhamānai vā hatai vā hañ‍ñamānai vā nābhijānāmi cittassa añ‍ñathatta’’nti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ, nandamātai, abbhutaṃ nandamātai! yatra hi nāma cittuppādampi cittuppādamattampi (syā॰) parisaudhaissasī’’ti|

‘‘na khau mai, bhantai, aisaiva acchariyau abbhutau  dhammau| atthi mai añ‍ñaupi acchariyau abbhutau dhammau| idha mai, bhantai, sāmikau kālaṅkatau añ‍ñataraṃ yakkhayauniṃ upapan‍nau| sau mai tainaiva purimaina attabhāvaina uddassaisi| na khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, abhijānāmi  tataunidānaṃ cittassa añ‍ñathatta’’nti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ, nandamātai, abbhutaṃ, nandamātai! yatra hi nāma cittuppādampi parisaudhaissasī’’ti|

‘‘na khau mai, bhantai, aisaiva acchariyau abbhutau dhammau| atthi mai añ‍ñaupi acchariyau abbhutau dhammau| yatauhaṃ, bhantai, sāmikassa daharassaiva daharā ānītā nābhijānāmi sāmikaṃ manasāpi aticaritā aticarituṃ (syā॰), aticārittaṃ (ka॰), kutau pana kāyainā’’ti! ‘‘acchariyaṃ, nandamātai, abbhutaṃ, nandamātai! yatra hi nāma cittuppādampi parisaudhaissasī’’ti|

‘‘na  khau mai, bhantai, aisaiva acchariyau abbhutau dhammau| atthi mai añ‍ñaupi acchariyau abbhutau dhammau| yadāhaṃ, bhantai, upāsikā paṭidaisitā nābhijānāmi kiñ‍ci sikkhāpadaṃ sañ‍cic‍ca vītik‍kamitā’’ti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ, nandamātai, abbhutaṃ, nandamātai’’ti!

‘‘na khau mai, bhantai, aisaiva acchariyau abbhutau dhammau| atthi mai añ‍ñaupi acchariyau abbhutau dhammau| idhāhaṃ, bhantai, yāvadaiva yāvadaiva (sī॰ syā॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.152 pāḷi ca aṭṭhakathāṭīkā ca passitabbā ākaṅkhāmi vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhikā ca viharāmi satā ca sampajānā sukhañ‍ca  kāyai  paṭisaṃvaidaimi, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’’ti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ, nandamātai, abbhutaṃ, nandamātai’’ti!

‘‘na khau mai, bhantai, aisaiva acchariyau abbhutau dhammau| atthi mai añ‍ñaupi acchariyau abbhutau dhammau| yānimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā daisitāni pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni nāhaṃ taisaṃ kiñ‍ci attani appahīnaṃ samanupassāmī’’ti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ, nandamātai, abbhutaṃ, nandamātai’’ti!

atha  khau āyasmā sāriputtau nandamātaraṃ upāsikaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmīti| dasamaṃ|

mahāyañ‍ñavaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

ṭhiti ca parikkhāraṃ dvai, aggī sañ‍ñā ca dvai parā|

maithunā saṃyaugau dānaṃ, nandamātaina tai dasāti||

paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



6. abyākatavaggau

1. abyākatasuttaṃ

54. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ  nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina sutavatau ariyasāvakassa vicikicchā nuppaj‍jati abyākatavatthūsū’’ti?

‘‘diṭṭhiniraudhā khau, bhikkhu, sutavatau ariyasāvakassa vicikicchā nuppaj‍jati abyākatavatthūsu| ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, bhikkhu, diṭṭhigatamaitaṃ; ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, bhikkhu, diṭṭhigatamaitaṃ; ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, bhikkhu, diṭṭhigatamaitaṃ; ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, bhikkhu, diṭṭhigatamaitaṃ| assutavā, bhikkhu, puthuj‍janau diṭṭhiṃ nappajānāti, diṭṭhisamudayaṃ nappajānāti, diṭṭhiniraudhaṃ nappajānāti, diṭṭhiniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānāti| tassa sā diṭṭhi pavaḍḍhati, sau na parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, na parimuc‍cati dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘sutavā ca khau, bhikkhu, ariyasāvakau diṭṭhiṃ pajānāti, diṭṭhisamudayaṃ pajānāti, diṭṭhiniraudhaṃ pajānāti, diṭṭhiniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti| tassa sā diṭṭhi nirujjhati, sau parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi  daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, parimuc‍cati dukkhasmāti vadāmi| aivaṃ jānaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau aivaṃ passaṃ ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na byākarauti; ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na byākarauti; ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na byākarauti; ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na byākarauti| aivaṃ jānaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau aivaṃ passaṃ aivaṃ abyākaraṇadhammau hauti abyākatavatthūsu| aivaṃ jānaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau aivaṃ passaṃ na chambhati, na kampati, na vaidhati, na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati abyākatavatthūsu|

‘‘‘hauti  tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, bhikkhu, taṇhāgatamaitaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñāgatamaitaṃ …pai॰… mañ‍ñitamaitaṃ…pai॰… papañ‍citamaitaṃ…pai॰… upādānagatamaitaṃ…pai॰… ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, bhikkhu, vippaṭisārau aisau; ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, bhikkhu, vippaṭisārau aisau; ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, bhikkhu, vippaṭisārau aisau; ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti khau, bhikkhu, vippaṭisārau aisau| assutavā, bhikkhu, puthuj‍janau vippaṭisāraṃ nappajānāti, vippaṭisārasamudayaṃ nappajānāti, vippaṭisāraniraudhaṃ nappajānāti, vippaṭisāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānāti| tassa sau vippaṭisārau pavaḍḍhati, sau na parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, na parimuc‍cati dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘sutavā ca khau, bhikkhu, ariyasāvakau vippaṭisāraṃ pajānāti, vippaṭisārasamudayaṃ pajānāti, vippaṭisāraniraudhaṃ pajānāti, vippaṭisāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti| tassa sau vippaṭisārau nirujjhati, sau parimuc‍cati jātiyā…pai॰… dukkhasmāti vadāmi| aivaṃ jānaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau aivaṃ passaṃ ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na byākarauti…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’tipi na byākarauti| aivaṃ jānaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau aivaṃ passaṃ aivaṃ abyākaraṇadhammau hauti abyākatavatthūsu| aivaṃ jānaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau aivaṃ passaṃ na chambhati, na kampati, na vaidhati, na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati abyākatavatthūsu| ayaṃ  khau, bhikkhu, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina sutavatau ariyasāvakassa vicikicchā nuppaj‍jati abyākatavatthūsū’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. purisagatisuttaṃ

55. ‘‘satta ca satta (sī॰), satta ca khau (ka॰), bhikkhavai, purisagatiyau daisaissāmi anupādā ca parinibbānaṃ parinibbāṇaṃ (sī॰)| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, satta purisagatiyau?

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – ‘nau cassa nau ca mai siyā, na bhavissati na mai bhavissati, yadatthi yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ pajahāmī’ti upaikkhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau bhavai na raj‍jati, sambhavai na raj‍jati, atthuttari padaṃ santaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| tañ‍ca khvassa padaṃ na sabbaina sabbaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti, tassa na sabbaina sabbaṃ mānānusayau pahīnau hauti, na sabbaina sabbaṃ bhavarāgānusayau pahīnau hauti, na sabbaina sabbaṃ avij‍jānusayau pahīnau hauti| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, divasaṃsantattai divasasantattai (sī॰ syā॰) ma॰ ni॰ 2.154 ayaukapālai hañ‍ñamānai papaṭikā nibbattitvā nibbāyaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – ‘nau cassa nau ca mai siyā, na bhavissati na mai bhavissati, yadatthi yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ pajahāmī’ti upaikkhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau bhavai  na raj‍jati, sambhavai na raj‍jati, atthuttari padaṃ santaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| tañ‍ca khvassa padaṃ na sabbaina  sabbaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti, tassa na sabbaina sabbaṃ mānānusayau pahīnau hauti, na sabbaina sabbaṃ bhavarāgānusayau pahīnau hauti, na sabbaina sabbaṃ avij‍jānusayau pahīnau hauti| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – ‘nau cassa nau ca mai siyā, na bhavissati na mai bhavissati, yadatthi yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ pajahāmī’ti upaikkhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau bhavai na raj‍jati, sambhavai na raj‍jati, atthuttari padaṃ santaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| tañ‍ca khvassa padaṃ na sabbaina sabbaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti, tassa na sabbaina sabbaṃ mānānusayau pahīnau hauti, na sabbaina sabbaṃ bhavarāgānusayau pahīnau hauti, na sabbaina sabbaṃ avij‍jānusayau pahīnau hauti| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, divasaṃsantattai ayaukapālai hañ‍ñamānai papaṭikā nibbattitvā uppatitvā  nibbāyaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – nau cassa nau ca mai siyā…pai॰… sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – nau cassa nau ca mai siyā…pai॰… sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, divasaṃsantattai ayaukapālai hañ‍ñamānai papaṭikā nibbattitvā uppatitvā anupahac‍ca talaṃ nibbāyaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – nau cassa nau ca mai siyā…pai॰…  sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – nau cassa nau ca mai siyā…pai॰… sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, divasaṃsantattai ayaukapālai hañ‍ñamānai papaṭikā nibbattitvā uppatitvā upahac‍ca talaṃ nibbāyaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – nau cassa nau ca mai siyā…pai॰… sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – nau cassa nau ca mai siyā…pai॰… sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, divasaṃsantattai ayaukapālai hañ‍ñamānai papaṭikā nibbattitvā uppatitvā parittai tiṇapuñ‍jai vā kaṭṭhapuñ‍jai vā nipataiyya| sā tattha aggimpi janaiyya, dhūmampi janaiyya, aggimpi janaitvā dhūmampi janaitvā tamaiva parittaṃ tiṇapuñ‍jaṃ vā kaṭṭhapuñ‍jaṃ vā pariyādiyitvā anāhārā nibbāyaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – nau cassa nau ca mai siyā…pai॰… sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – nau cassa nau ca mai siyā…pai॰… sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, divasaṃsantattai ayaukapālai hañ‍ñamānai papaṭikā nibbattitvā uppatitvā  vipulai tiṇapuñ‍jai vā kaṭṭhapuñ‍jai vā nipataiyya| sā tattha aggimpi janaiyya, dhūmampi janaiyya, aggimpi janaitvā dhūmampi janaitvā tamaiva vipulaṃ tiṇapuñ‍jaṃ vā kaṭṭhapuñ‍jaṃ vā  pariyādiyitvā anāhārā nibbāyaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – nau cassa nau ca mai siyā…pai॰… sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – ‘nau cassa nau ca mai siyā, na bhavissati na mai bhavissati, yadatthi yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ pajahāmī’ti upaikkhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau bhavai na raj‍jati, sambhavai na raj‍jati, atthuttari padaṃ santaṃ sammapañ‍ñāya passati| tañ‍ca khvassa padaṃ na sabbaina sabbaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti, tassa na sabbaina sabbaṃ mānānusayau pahīnau hauti, na sabbaina sabbaṃ bhavarāgānusayau pahīnau hauti, na sabbaina sabbaṃ avij‍jānusayau pahīnau hauti| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, divasaṃsantattai ayaukapālai hañ‍ñamānai papaṭikā nibbattitvā uppatitvā mahantai tiṇapuñ‍jai vā kaṭṭhapuñ‍jai vā nipataiyya| sā tattha aggimpi janaiyya, dhūmampi janaiyya, aggimpi janaitvā dhūmampi janaitvā tamaiva mahantaṃ tiṇapuñ‍jaṃ  vā kaṭṭhapuñ‍jaṃ vā pariyādiyitvā gacchampi dahaiyya ḍahaiyya (añ‍ñattha), dāyampi dahaiyya, gacchampi dahitvā dāyampi dahitvā haritantaṃ vā pathantaṃ vā panthantaṃ vā (sī॰) syāmapautthakai idaṃ na dissati sailantaṃ vā udakantaṃ vā ramaṇīyaṃ vā bhūmibhāgaṃ āgamma anāhārā nibbāyaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – nau cassa nau ca mai siyā…pai॰… sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī| imā khau, bhikkhavai, satta purisagatiyau|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anupādāparinibbānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti – ‘nau cassa nau ca mai siyā, na bhavissati na mai bhavissati, yadatthi yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ pajahāmī’ti upaikkhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau bhavai na raj‍jati, sambhavai na raj‍jati, atthuttari  padaṃ santaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| tañ‍ca khvassa padaṃ sabbaina sabbaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti, tassa sabbaina sabbaṃ mānānusayau pahīnau hauti, sabbaina sabbaṃ bhavarāgānusayau pahīnau hauti, sabbaina sabbaṃ avij‍jānusayau pahīnau hauti| sau āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, anupādāparinibbānaṃ| imā khau, bhikkhavai, satta purisagatiyau anupādā ca parinibbāna’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. tissabrahmāsuttaṃ

56. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau dvai daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ gijjhakūṭaṃ  aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau aikā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitā, bhantai, bhikkhuniyau vimuttā’’ti| aparā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitā, bhantai, bhikkhuniyau anupādisaisā suvimuttā’’ti| idamavaucuṃ tā daivatā| samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| atha khau tā daivatā ‘‘samanuñ‍ñau satthā’’ti bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyiṃsu|

atha khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ dvai daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ gijjhakūṭaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, bhikkhavai, aikā daivatā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘aitā, bhantai, bhikkhuniyau vimuttā’ti| aparā daivatā maṃ aitadavauca  – ‘aitā, bhantai, bhikkhuniyau anupādisaisā suvimuttā’ti| idamavaucuṃ, bhikkhavai, tā daivatā| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyiṃsū’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavatau avidūrai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘katamaisānaṃ khau daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘saupādisaisai vā saupādisaisauti, anupādisaisai vā anupādisaisau’’’ti? taina khau pana samayaina tissau nāma bhikkhu adhunākālaṅkatau añ‍ñataraṃ brahmalaukaṃ upapan‍nau hauti| tatrāpi naṃ aivaṃ jānanti – ‘‘tissau brahmā mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaivaṃ – gijjhakūṭai pabbatai antarahitau tasmiṃ brahmalaukai pāturahausi| addasā khau tissau brahmā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ dūratauva  āgacchantaṃ| disvā āyasmantaṃ  mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna; svāgataṃ, mārisa mauggal‍lāna! cirassaṃ khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi, yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| nisīda, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, idamāsanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti| nisīdi khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau pañ‍ñattai āsanai| tissaupi khau brahmā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau tissaṃ brahmānaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca – ‘‘katamaisānaṃ khau, tissa, daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘saupādisaisai vā saupādisaisauti, anupādisaisai vā anupādisaisau’’’ti? ‘‘brahmakāyikānaṃ khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘saupādisaisai vā saupādisaisauti, anupādisaisai vā anupādisaisau’’’ti|

‘‘sabbaisañ‍ñaiva khau, tissa, brahmakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘saupādisaisai vā saupādisaisauti, anupādisaisai vā anupādisaisau’’’ti? ‘‘na khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, sabbaisaṃ brahmakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘saupādisaisai vā saupādisaisauti, anupādisaisai vā anupādisaisau’’’ti|

‘‘yai khau tai, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, brahmakāyikā daivā brahmaina āyunā santuṭṭhā brahmaina vaṇṇaina brahmaina sukhaina brahmaina yasaina brahmaina ādhipataiyyaina santuṭṭhā, tai uttari nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti| taisaṃ na aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘saupādisaisai vā saupādisaisauti, anupādisaisai vā anupādisaisau’ti| yai ca khau tai, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, brahmakāyikā daivā brahmaina āyunā asantuṭṭhā, brahmaina vaṇṇaina brahmaina sukhaina brahmaina yasaina brahmaina ādhipataiyyaina asantuṭṭhā, tai ca uttari nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ  pajānanti| taisaṃ aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘saupādisaisai vā saupādisaisauti, anupādisaisai vā anupādisaisau’’’ti|

‘‘idha, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhu ubhataubhāgavimuttau hauti| tamainaṃ tai daivā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā ubhataubhāgavimuttau| yāvassa kāyau ṭhassati tāva naṃ dakkhanti daivamanussā| kāyassa bhaidā na naṃ dakkhanti  daivamanussā’ti| aivampi khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, taisaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘saupādisaisai vā saupādisaisauti, anupādisaisai vā anupādisaisau’’’ti|

‘‘idha pana, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhu pañ‍ñāvimuttau hauti| tamainaṃ tai daivā aivaṃ jānanti  – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā pañ‍ñāvimuttau| yāvassa kāyau ṭhassati tāva naṃ dakkhanti daivamanussā| kāyassa bhaidā na naṃ dakkhanti daivamanussā’ti| aivampi khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, taisaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘saupādisaisai vā saupādisaisauti, anupādisaisai vā anupādisaisau’’’ti|

‘‘idha pana, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhu kāyasakkhī hauti| tamainaṃ daivā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā kāyasakkhī| appaiva nāma ayamāyasmā anulaumikāni saināsanāni paṭisaivamānau kalyāṇamittai bhajamānau indriyāni saman‍nānayamānau – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’ti| aivampi khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, taisaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘saupādisaisai vā saupādisaisauti, anupādisaisai vā anupādisaisau’’’ti|

‘‘idha pana, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhu diṭṭhippattau hauti…pai॰… saddhāvimuttau hauti…pai॰… dhammānusārī hauti| tamainaṃ tai daivā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā dhammānusārī| appaiva nāma ayamāyasmā anulaumikāni saināsanāni paṭisaivamānau kalyāṇamittai bhajamānau indriyāni saman‍nānayamānau – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva  dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’ti| aivampi khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, taisaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘saupādisaisai vā saupādisaisauti, anupādisaisai vā anupādisaisau’’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tissassa brahmunau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaivaṃ – brahmalaukai antarahitau gijjhakūṭai pabbatai pāturahausi| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau yāvatakau ahausi tissaina brahmunā saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi|

‘‘na  hi pana tai, mauggal‍lāna, tissau brahmā sattamaṃ animittavihāriṃ puggalaṃ daisaiti’’| ‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau; aitassa, sugata, kālau! yaṃ bhagavā sattamaṃ animittavihāriṃ puggalaṃ daisaiyya| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, mauggal‍lāna, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘idha, mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhu sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tamainaṃ tai daivā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| appaiva nāma ayamāyasmā anulaumikāni saināsanāni paṭisaivamānau kalyāṇamittai bhajamānau  indriyāni saman‍nānayamānau – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā  upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’ti| aivaṃ khau, mauggal‍lāna, taisaṃ daivānaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘saupādisaisai vā saupādisaisauti, anupādisaisai vā anupādisaisau’’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. sīhasaināpatisuttaṃ

57. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau sīhau saināpati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sīhau saināpati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sak‍kā nu khau, bhantai, sandiṭṭhikaṃ dānaphalaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitu’’nti?

‘‘taina hi, sīha, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi, yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sīha, idha dvai purisā – aikau purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, aikau purisau saddhau dānapati  anuppadānaratau| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sīha, kaṃ nu khau kiṃ nu khau (ka॰) arahantau paṭhamaṃ anukampantā anukampaiyyuṃ – ‘yau vā sau purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, yau vā sau purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau’’’ti?

‘‘yau  sau, bhantai, purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, kintaṃ kinti (ka॰) arahantau paṭhamaṃ anukampantā anukampissanti! yau ca khau sau, bhantai, purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau taṃyaiva arahantau paṭhamaṃ anukampantā anukampaiyyuṃ’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sīha, kaṃ nu khau  arahantau paṭhamaṃ upasaṅkamantā upasaṅkamaiyyuṃ – ‘yau vā sau purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, yau vā sau purisau  saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau’’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, kintaṃ arahantau paṭhamaṃ upasaṅkamantā upasaṅkamissanti! yau ca khau sau, bhantai, purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau taṃyaiva arahantau paṭhamaṃ upasaṅkamantā upasaṅkamaiyyuṃ’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sīha, kassa nu khau arahantau paṭhamaṃ paṭiggaṇhantā paṭiggaṇhaiyyuṃ – ‘yau vā sau purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, yau vā sau purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau’’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, kintaṃ tassa arahantau paṭhamaṃ paṭiggaṇhantā paṭiggaṇhissanti! yau ca khau sau, bhantai, purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau tassaiva arahantau paṭhamaṃ paṭiggaṇhantā paṭiggaṇhaiyyuṃ’’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sīha, kassa nu khau arahantau paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintā daisaiyyuṃ – ‘yau vā sau purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, yau vā sau purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau’’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, kintaṃ tassa arahantau paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintā daisaissanti! yau ca khau sau, bhantai, purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau tassaiva arahantau paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintā daisaiyyuṃ’’|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sīha, kassa nu khau kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchaiyya – ‘yau vā sau purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, yau vā sau purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau’’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, kintaṃ tassa kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchissati! yau ca khau sau, bhantai, purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau tassaiva kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchaiyya’’|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sīha, kau nu khau yaṃyadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamaiyya, yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ visāradau  upasaṅkamaiyya amaṅkubhūtau – ‘yau vā sau purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, yau vā sau purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau’’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, kiṃ sau yaṃyadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamissati, yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ visāradau upasaṅkamissati amaṅkubhūtau! yau ca khau sau, bhantai, purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau sau yaṃyadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamaiyya, yadi khattiyaparisaṃ yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ visāradau upasaṅkamaiyya amaṅkubhūtau’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sīha, kau nu khau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya – ‘yau vā sau purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, yau vā sau purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau’’’ti? ‘‘yau sau, bhantai, purisau assaddhau maccharī kadariyau paribhāsakau, kiṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissati! yau ca khau sau, bhantai, purisau saddhau dānapati anuppadānaratau sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’’|

‘‘yānimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā sandiṭṭhikāni dānaphalāni akkhātāni, nāhaṃ aittha bhagavatau saddhāya gacchāmi| ahampi aitāni jānāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai, dāyakau dānapati, maṃ arahantau paṭhamaṃ anukampantā anukampanti| ahaṃ, bhantai, dāyakau dānapati, maṃ arahantau paṭhamaṃ upasaṅkamantā upasaṅkamanti| ahaṃ, bhantai, dāyakau dānapati, mayhaṃ arahantau paṭhamaṃ paṭiggaṇhantā paṭiggaṇhanti| ahaṃ, bhantai, dāyakau dānapati, mayhaṃ arahantau paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintā daisainti| ahaṃ, bhantai, dāyakau dānapati, mayhaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘sīhau saināpati dāyakau kārakau saṅghupaṭṭhākau’ti| ahaṃ, bhantai, dāyakau dānapati yaṃyadaiva parisaṃ upasaṅkamāmi, yadi khattiyaparisaṃ…pai॰… yadi samaṇaparisaṃ visāradau upasaṅkamāmi amaṅkubhūtau| yānimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā sandiṭṭhikāni dānaphalāni akkhātāni, nāhaṃ aittha bhagavatau saddhāya gacchāmi| ahampi aitāni jānāmi| yañ‍ca khau maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā aivamāha – ‘dāyakau, sīha, dānapati kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’ti| aitāhaṃ na jānāmi, aittha ca panāhaṃ, bhagavatau saddhāya gacchāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, sīha; aivamaitaṃ, sīha! dāyakau, sīha, dānapati kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. arakkhaiyyasuttaṃ

58. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa arakkhaiyyāni, tīhi ca anupavaj‍jau| katamāni cattāri tathāgatassa arakkhaiyyāni? parisuddhakāyasamācārau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau; natthi tathāgatassa kāyaduc‍caritaṃ yaṃ tathāgatau rakkhaiyya – ‘mā mai idaṃ parau añ‍ñāsī’ti| parisuddhavacīsamācārau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau; natthi tathāgatassa vacīduc‍caritaṃ yaṃ tathāgatau rakkhaiyya – ‘mā mai idaṃ parau añ‍ñāsī’ti| parisuddhamanausamācārau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau; natthi tathāgatassa manauduc‍caritaṃ yaṃ tathāgatau rakkhaiyya – ‘mā mai idaṃ parau añ‍ñāsī’ti| parisuddhājīvau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau; natthi tathāgatassa micchāājīvau yaṃ tathāgatau rakkhaiyya – ‘mā mai idaṃ parau añ‍ñāsī’ti| imāni cattāri tathāgatassa arakkhaiyyāni|

‘‘katamaihi tīhi anupavaj‍jau? svākkhātadhammau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau| tatra vata maṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmiṃ sahadhammaina paṭicaudaissati – ‘itipi tvaṃ na svākkhātadhammau’ti| nimittamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, na samanupassāmi| aitamahaṃ aitaṃpahaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), bhikkhavai, nimittaṃ asamanupassantau khaimappattau abhayappattau vaisāraj‍jappattau viharāmi|

‘‘supañ‍ñattā khau pana mai, bhikkhavai, sāvakānaṃ nibbānagāminī paṭipadā| yathāpaṭipan‍nā mama sāvakā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai  sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti| tatra vata maṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmiṃ sahadhammaina paṭicaudaissati – ‘itipi tai na supañ‍ñattā sāvakānaṃ nibbānagāminī paṭipadā| yathāpaṭipan‍nā tava sāvakā āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’ti| nimittamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, na samanupassāmi| aitamahaṃ, bhikkhavai, nimittaṃ asamanupassantau khaimappattau abhayappattau vaisāraj‍jappattau viharāmi|

‘‘anaikasatā khau pana mai, bhikkhavai, sāvakaparisā āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti| tatra vata maṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmiṃ sahadhammaina paṭicaudaissati – ‘itipi tai na anaikasatā sāvakaparisā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’ti| nimittamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, na samanupassāmi| aitamahaṃ, bhikkhavai, nimittaṃ  asamanupassantau khaimappattau abhayappattau vaisāraj‍jappattau viharāmi| imaihi tīhi anupavaj‍jau|

‘‘imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri tathāgatassa arakkhaiyyāni, imaihi ca tīhi anupavaj‍jau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kimilasuttaṃ

59. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kimilāyaṃ viharati niculavanai vaiḷuvanai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.202; 7.40| atha khau āyasmā kimilau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā kimilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idha, kimila, tathāgatai parinibbutai bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau satthari agāravā viharanti appatissā, dhammai agāravā viharanti appatissā, saṅghai agāravā viharanti appatissā, sikkhāya agāravā viharanti appatissā, samādhismiṃ agāravā viharanti appatissā, appamādai agāravā viharanti appatissā, paṭisanthārai agāravā viharanti appatissā| ayaṃ khau, kimila, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau na ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti|

‘‘kau  pana, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, kimila, tathāgatai parinibbutai bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau satthari sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, dhammai sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, saṅghai sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, sikkhāya sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, samādhismiṃ sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, appamādai sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, paṭisanthārai sagāravā viharanti sappatissā| ayaṃ khau, kimila, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yaina tathāgatai parinibbutai saddhammau ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sattadhammasuttaṃ

60. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nacirassaiva āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya| katamaihi sattahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau hauti, sīlavā hauti, bahussutau hauti, paṭisal‍līnau hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, satimā hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nacirassaiva āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. pacalāyamānasuttaṃ

61. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau magadhaisu kal‍lavāḷaputtagāmai kal‍lavālamuttagāmai (syā॰) pacalāyamānau nisin‍nau  hauti| addasā khau bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ magadhaisu kal‍lavāḷaputtagāmai pacalāyamānaṃ nisin‍naṃ| disvā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaivaṃ – bhaggaisu susumāragirai bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai antarahitau magadhaisu kal‍lavāḷaputtagāmai āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa sammukhai pāturahausi| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘pacalāyasi nau tvaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, pacalāyasi nau tvaṃ, mauggal‍lānā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘tasmātiha, mauggal‍lāna, yathāsañ‍ñissa tai viharatau taṃ middhaṃ auk‍kamati, taṃ sañ‍ñaṃ mā manasākāsi mā manasikāsi (sī॰), manasi karaiyyāsi (syā॰), manasākāsi (ka॰), taṃ  sañ‍ñaṃ mā bahulamakāsi taṃ sañ‍ñaṃ bahulaṃ karaiyyāsi (syā॰), taṃ sañ‍ñaṃ bahulamakāsi (ka॰)| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, vij‍jati yaṃ tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha’’|

‘‘nau cai tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha, tatau tvaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ caitasā anuvitak‍kaiyyāsi anuvicāraiyyāsi, manasā  anupaikkhaiyyāsi| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha|

‘‘nau cai tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha, tatau tvaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina sajjhāyaṃ karaiyyāsi| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha|

‘‘nau cai tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha, tatau tvaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, ubhau kaṇṇasautāni āviñchaiyyāsi āviñ‍jaiyyāsi (sī॰ syā॰), pāṇinā gattāni anumaj‍jaiyyāsi| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha|

‘‘nau cai tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha, tatau tvaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, uṭṭhāyāsanā udakaina akkhīni anumaj‍jitvā paniñ‍jitvā (ka॰) disā anuvilaukaiyyāsi, nakkhattāni tārakarūpāni ul‍laukaiyyāsi| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha|

‘‘nau cai tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha, tatau tvaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karaiyyāsi, divāsañ‍ñaṃ adhiṭṭhahaiyyāsi – yathā divā tathā rattiṃ yathā rattiṃ tathā divā| iti vivaṭaina vivaṭṭaina (syā॰), middhavigataina (ka॰) caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiyyāsi| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha|

‘‘nau  cai tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha, tatau tvaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, pacchāpuraisañ‍ñī pacchāpurai tathāsañ‍ñī (katthaci) caṅkamaṃ adhiṭṭhahaiyyāsi antaugataihi indriyaihi abahigataina mānasaina| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha|

‘‘nau  cai tai aivaṃ viharatau taṃ middhaṃ pahīyaitha, tatau tvaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, dakkhiṇaina  passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiyyāsi pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karitvā| paṭibuddhaina ca paṭibuddhainaiva (syā॰) tai, mauggal‍lāna, khippañ‍ñaiva pac‍cuṭṭhātabbaṃ – ‘na saiyyasukhaṃ na passasukhaṃ na middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharissāmī’ti| aivañhi tai, mauggal‍lāna, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha, mauggal‍lāna, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na uc‍cāsauṇḍaṃ paggahaitvā kulāni upasaṅkamissāmī’ti| aivañhi tai, mauggal‍lāna, sikkhitabbaṃ| sacai, mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhu uc‍cāsauṇḍaṃ paggahaitvā kulāni upasaṅkamati, santi hi, mauggal‍lāna, kulaisu kic‍cakaraṇīyāni| yaihi manussā āgataṃ bhikkhuṃ na manasi karaunti, tatra bhikkhussa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kausu nāma idāni maṃ imasmiṃ kulai paribhindi, virattarūpā dānimai mayi manussā’ti| itissa alābhaina maṅkubhāvau, maṅkubhūtassa uddhac‍caṃ, uddhatassa asaṃvarau, asaṃvutassa ārā cittaṃ samādhimhā|

‘‘tasmātiha, mauggal‍lāna, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na viggāhikakathaṃ kathaissāmī’ti| aivañhi tai, mauggal‍lāna, sikkhitabbaṃ| viggāhikāya, mauggal‍lāna, kathāya sati  kathābāhul‍laṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ, kathābāhul‍lai sati uddhac‍caṃ, uddhatassa asaṃvarau, asaṃvutassa ārā cittaṃ samādhimhā nāhaṃ mauggalāna sabbaihaiva samaggaṃ vaṇṇayāmi gahaṭṭhaihi| pabbajitaihi khau ahaṃ mauggal‍lāna samaggaṃ vaṇṇayāmi (ka॰)| nāhaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, sabbaihaiva saṃsaggaṃ vaṇṇayāmi| na panāhaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, sabbaihaiva saṃsaggaṃ na vaṇṇayāmi| sagahaṭṭhapabbajitaihi khau ahaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, saṃsaggaṃ na vaṇṇayāmi nāhaṃ mauggal‍lāna sabbaihaiva samaggaṃ vaṇṇayāmi gahaṭṭhaihi, pabbajitaihi khau ahaṃ mauggal‍lāna samaggaṃ vaṇṇayāmi (ka॰)| yāni ca khau tāni saināsanāni appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni manussarāhasaiyyakāni (sī॰ syā॰) paṭisal‍lānasāruppāni tathārūpaihi  saināsanaihi saṃsaggaṃ samaggaṃ (ka॰) vaṇṇayāmī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, bhikkhu saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttau hauti ac‍cantaniṭṭhau ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussāna’’nti?

‘‘idha, mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhunau sutaṃ hauti – ‘sabbai dhammā nālaṃ abhinivaisāyā’ti; aivañ‍caitaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhunau sutaṃ hauti – ‘sabbai dhammā nālaṃ abhinivaisāyā’ti| sau sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānāti, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya sabbaṃ dhammaṃ parijānāti| sabbaṃ dhammaṃ pariñ‍ñāya  yaṃkiñ‍ci vaidanaṃ vaidiyati sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā| sau tāsu vaidanāsu anic‍cānupassī viharati, virāgānupassī viharati, niraudhānupassī viharati, paṭinissaggānupassī viharati| sau tāsu vaidanāsu anic‍cānupassī viharantau virāgānupassī viharantau niraudhānupassī viharantau paṭinissaggānupassī viharantau na kiñ‍ci na ca kiñ‍ci (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 1.390 passitabbaṃ laukai upādiyati, anupādiyaṃ na paritassati, aparitassaṃ pac‍cattaṃyaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| aittāvatā khau, mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhu saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttau hauti ac‍cantaniṭṭhau ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussāna’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. maittasuttaṃ

62. itivu॰ 22 itivuttakaipi ‘‘mā, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñānaṃ bhāyittha| sukhassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, adhivacanaṃ yadidaṃ puñ‍ñāni yadidaṃ puñ‍ñanti (sī॰), yadidaṃ puñ‍ñāni (ka॰)| abhijānāmi khau panāhaṃ bhikkhavai dīgharattaṃ iṭṭhaṃ (syā॰), bhikkhavai dīgharattaṃ puñ‍ñānaṃ iṭṭhaṃ (?), bhikkhavai, dīgharattaṃ katānaṃ puñ‍ñānaṃ dīgharattaṃ iṭṭhaṃ bhikkhavai dīgharattaṃ iṭṭhaṃ (syā॰), bhikkhavai dīgharattaṃ puñ‍ñānaṃ iṭṭhaṃ (?) kantaṃ manāpaṃ vipākaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ| satta vassāni maittaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaisiṃ| satta vassāni maittaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaitvā satta saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai nayimaṃ laukaṃ punāgamāsiṃ| saṃvaṭṭamānai sudāhaṃ saṃvaṭṭamānassudāhaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, laukai ābhassarūpagau haumi, vivaṭṭamānai laukai suñ‍ñaṃ brahmavimānaṃ upapaj‍jāmi|

‘‘tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmā haumi mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī| chattiṃsakkhattuṃ khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau ahausiṃ daivānamindau; anaikasatakkhattuṃ rājā ahausiṃ cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imāni satta ratanāni ahaisuṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – cak‍karatanaṃ, hatthiratanaṃ, assaratanaṃ, maṇiratanaṃ, itthiratanaṃ, gahapatiratanaṃ, pariṇāyakaratanamaiva sattamaṃ| parausahassaṃ khau pana mai, bhikkhavai, puttā ahaisuṃ sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina abhivijiya ajjhāvasi’’nti ajjhāvasanti (syā॰) ajjhāvasati (sī॰ ka॰)|

‘‘passa puñ‍ñānaṃ vipākaṃ, kusalānaṃ sukhaisinau sukhaisinaṃ (sī॰)|

maittaṃ cittaṃ vibhāvaitvā, satta vassāni bhikkhavau bhikkhavai (ka॰)|

sattasaṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai, nayimaṃ laukaṃ punāgamiṃ punāgamaṃ (syā॰)||

‘‘saṃvaṭṭamānai  laukamhi, haumi ābhassarūpagau|

vivaṭṭamānai laukasmiṃ, suñ‍ñabrahmūpagau ahuṃ||

‘‘sattakkhattuṃ mahābrahmā, vasavattī tadā ahuṃ|

chattiṃsakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ||

‘‘cak‍kavattī  ahuṃ rājā, jambumaṇḍassa jambudīpassa (sī॰), jambusaṇḍassa (syā॰) issarau|

muddhāvasittau  muddhābhisittau (ka॰) khattiyau, manussādhipatī ahuṃ||

‘‘adaṇḍaina asatthaina, vijaiyya pathaviṃ imaṃ|

asāhasaina kammaina dhammaina (sī॰ syā॰), samaina anusāsi taṃ||

‘‘dhammaina raj‍jaṃ kāraitvā, asmiṃ pathavimaṇḍalai|

mahaddhanai mahābhaugai, aḍḍhai ajāyihaṃ kulai||

‘‘sabbakāmaihi sampan‍nai sampuṇṇai (ka॰), ratanaihi ca sattahi|

buddhā saṅgāhakā laukai, taihi aitaṃ sudaisitaṃ||

‘‘aisau haitu mahantassa, pathabyau mai na vipaj‍jati aisa haitu mahantassa, puthabyau yaina vuc‍cati (sī॰ syā॰)|

pahūtavittūpakaraṇau, rājā hauti haumi (sī॰ syā॰) patāpavā||

‘‘iddhimā yasavā hauti haumi (sī॰ syā॰), jambumaṇḍassa jambusaṇḍassa (sī॰ syā॰) issarau|

kau sutvā nappasīdaiyya, api kaṇhābhijātiyau||

‘‘tasmā  hi attakāmaina atthakāmaina (syā॰ ka॰), mahattamabhikaṅkhatā|

saddhammau garukātabbau, saraṃ buddhānasāsana’’nti|| navamaṃ|

10. bhariyāsuttaṃ

63. atha  khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| taina khau pana samayaina anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivaisanai manussā uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā haunti| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ  khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ  nu tai, gahapati, nivaisanai manussā uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā kaivaṭṭā mañ‍ñai macchavilaupai’’ti? ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, sujātā gharasuṇhā aḍḍhakulā ānītā| sā naiva sassuṃ ādiyati, na sasuraṃ ādiyati, na sāmikaṃ ādiyati, bhagavantampi na sak‍karauti na garuṃ karauti na mānaiti na pūjaitī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā sujātaṃ gharasuṇhaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi, sujātai’’ti! ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sujātā gharasuṇhā bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau sujātaṃ gharasuṇhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca  –

‘‘satta khau imā, sujātai, purisassa bhariyāyau| katamā satta? vadhakasamā, caurīsamā, ayyasamā, mātāsamā, bhaginīsamā, sakhīsamā, dāsīsamā| imā khau, sujātai, satta purisassa bhariyāyau| tāsaṃ tvaṃ katamā’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ nāhaṃ (syā॰), bhantai, imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yathāhaṃ imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ jānaiyya’’nti| ‘‘taina hi, sujātai, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sujātā gharasuṇhā bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘paduṭṭhacittā  ahitānukampinī,

añ‍ñaisu rattā atimañ‍ñatai patiṃ|

dhanaina kītassa vadhāya ussukā,

yā aivarūpā purisassa bhariyā|

‘vadhā ca bhariyā’ti ca sā pavuc‍cati||

‘‘yaṃ itthiyā vindati sāmikau dhanaṃ,

sippaṃ vaṇij‍jañ‍ca kasiṃ adhiṭṭhahaṃ|

appampi tassa apahātumicchati,

yā  aivarūpā purisassa bhariyā|

‘caurī ca bhariyā’ti ca sā pavuc‍cati||

‘‘akammakāmā  alasā mahagghasā,

pharusā ca caṇḍī duruttavādinī|

uṭṭhāyakānaṃ abhibhuyya vattati,

yā aivarūpā purisassa bhariyā|

‘ayyā ca bhariyā’ti ca sā pavuc‍cati||

‘‘yā  sabbadā hauti hitānukampinī,

mātāva puttaṃ anurakkhatai patiṃ|

tatau dhanaṃ sambhatamassa rakkhati,

yā aivarūpā purisassa bhariyā|

‘mātā ca bhariyā’ti ca sā pavuc‍cati||

‘‘yathāpi jaiṭṭhā bhaginī kaniṭṭhakā kaṇiṭṭhā (sī॰), kaniṭṭhā (syā॰),

sagāravā hauti sakamhi sāmikai|

hirīmanā  bhattuvasānuvattinī,

yā aivarūpā purisassa bhariyā|‘bhaginī ca bhariyā’ti ca sā pavuc‍cati||

‘‘yācīdha disvāna patiṃ pamaudati,

sakhī sakhāraṃva cirassamāgataṃ|

kaulaiyyakā sīlavatī patibbatā,

yā aivarūpā purisassa bhariyā|

‘sakhī ca bhariyā’ti ca sā pavuc‍cati||

‘‘ak‍kuddhasantā vadhadaṇḍataj‍jitā,

aduṭṭhacittā patinau titikkhati|

ak‍kaudhanā bhattuvasānuvattinī,

yā aivarūpā purisassa bhariyā|

‘dāsī ca bhariyā’ti ca sā pavuc‍cati||

‘‘yācīdha bhariyā vadhakāti vuc‍cati,

‘caurī ca ayyā’ti ca yā pavuc‍cati|

dussīlarūpā pharusā anādarā,

kāyassa bhaidā nirayaṃ vajanti tā||

‘‘yācīdha  mātā bhaginī sakhīti ca,

‘dāsī ca bhariyā’ti ca sā pavuc‍cati|

sīlai ṭhitattā cirarattasaṃvutā,

kāyassa bhaidā sugatiṃ vajanti tā’’ti||

‘‘imā  khau, sujātai, satta purisassa bhariyāyau| tāsaṃ tvaṃ katamā’’ti? ‘‘aj‍jataggai  maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dāsīsamaṃ sāmikassa bhariyaṃ dhāraitū’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. kaudhanasuttaṃ

64. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā sapattakantā sapattakaraṇā kaudhanaṃ āgacchanti itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā| katamai satta? idha, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa aivaṃ icchati – ‘ahau vatāyaṃ dubbaṇṇau assā’ti! taṃ kissa haitu? na, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa vaṇṇavatāya nandati| kaudhanauyaṃ kaudhanāyaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, purisapuggalau kaudhābhibhūtau kaudhaparaitau, kiñ‍cāpi sau hauti sunhātau suvilittau kappitakaisamassu audātavatthavasanau audātavasanau (ka॰); atha khau sau dubbaṇṇauva hauti kaudhābhibhūtau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau dhammau sapattakantau sapattakaraṇau kaudhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa aivaṃ icchati – ‘ahau vatāyaṃ dukkhaṃ sayaiyyā’ti! taṃ kissa haitu? na, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa sukhasaiyyāya nandati| kaudhanauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, purisapuggalau kaudhābhibhūtau kaudhaparaitau, kiñ‍cāpi sau pal‍laṅkai saiti gaunakatthatai paṭalikatthatai kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇai sauttaracchadai ubhataulauhitakūpadhānai; atha khau sau dukkhañ‍ñaiva saiti kaudhābhibhūtau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau dhammau sapattakantau sapattakaraṇau kaudhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa aivaṃ icchati – ‘ahau vatāyaṃ na pacuratthau assā’ti! taṃ kissa haitu? na, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa pacuratthatāya nandati| kaudhanauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, purisapuggalau kaudhābhibhūtau kaudhaparaitau, anatthampi gahaitvā ‘atthau mai gahitau’ti mañ‍ñati, atthampi gahaitvā ‘anatthau mai gahitau’ti mañ‍ñati| tassimai dhammā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vipac‍canīkā gahitā dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti kaudhābhibhūtassa| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau dhammau sapattakantau sapattakaraṇau kaudhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa aivaṃ icchati – ‘ahau vatāyaṃ na bhaugavā assā’ti! taṃ kissa haitu? na, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa bhaugavatāya nandati| kaudhanassa, bhikkhavai, purisapuggalassa kaudhābhibhūtassa kaudhaparaitassa, yaipissa tai haunti bhaugā uṭṭhānavīriyādhigatā  bāhābalaparicitā saidāvakkhittā dhammikā dhammaladdhā, taipi rājānau rājakausaṃ pavaisainti kaudhābhibhūtassa| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau dhammau sapattakantau sapattakaraṇau kaudhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa aivaṃ icchati – ‘ahau vatāyaṃ na yasavā assā’ti! taṃ kissa haitu? na, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa yasavatāya nandati| kaudhanauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, purisapuggalau kaudhābhibhūtau kaudhaparaitau, yaupissa sau hauti yasau appamādādhigatau, tamhāpi dhaṃsati kaudhābhibhūtau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau dhammau sapattakantau sapattakaraṇau kaudhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa aivaṃ icchati – ‘ahau vatāyaṃ na  mittavā assā’ti! taṃ kissa haitu? na, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa mittavatāya nandati| kaudhanaṃ, bhikkhavai, purisapuggalaṃ kaudhābhibhūtaṃ kaudhaparaitaṃ, yaipissa tai haunti mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā, taipi ārakā parivaj‍janti kaudhābhibhūtaṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaṭṭhau dhammau sapattakantau sapattakaraṇau kaudhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa aivaṃ icchati – ‘ahau vatāyaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyā’ti! taṃ kissa haitu? na, bhikkhavai, sapattau sapattassa sugatigamanai nandati| kaudhanauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, purisapuggalau kaudhābhibhūtau kaudhaparaitau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā vācāya…pai॰… kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati kaudhābhibhūtau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattamau dhammau sapattakantau sapattakaraṇau kaudhanaṃ āgacchati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta dhammā sapattakantā sapattakaraṇā kaudhanaṃ āgacchanti itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā’’ti|

‘‘kaudhanau dubbaṇṇau hauti, athau dukkhampi saiti sau|

athau atthaṃ gahaitvāna, anatthaṃ adhipaj‍jati adhigacchati (sī॰), paṭipaj‍jati (syā॰)||

‘‘tatau  kāyaina vācāya, vadhaṃ katvāna kaudhanau|

kaudhābhibhūtau purisau, dhanajāniṃ nigacchati||

‘‘kaudhasammadasammattau, āyasakyaṃ āyasakkhaṃ (syā॰) nigacchati|

ñātimittā suhaj‍jā ca, parivaj‍janti kaudhanaṃ||

itivu॰ 88 itivuttakaipi ‘‘anatthajananau kaudhau, kaudhau cittappakaupanau|

bhayamantaratau jātaṃ, taṃ janau nāvabujjhati||

‘‘kuddhau atthaṃ na jānāti, kuddhau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhatamaṃ tadā hauti, yaṃ kaudhau sahatai naraṃ||

‘‘yaṃ  kuddhau uparaudhaiti, sukaraṃ viya duk‍karaṃ|

pacchā sau vigatai kaudhai, aggidaḍḍhauva tappati||

‘‘dummaṅkuyaṃ padassaiti sadassaiti (sī॰), paṭhamaṃ dassaiti (syā॰), dhūmaṃ dhūmīva pāvakau|

yatau patāyati kaudhau, yaina kujjhanti mānavā||

‘‘nāssa hirī na auttappaṃ na assa hirī auttappañ‍ca (ka॰), na vācau hauti gāravau|

kaudhaina abhibhūtassa, na dīpaṃ hauti kiñ‍canaṃ||

‘‘tapanīyāni kammāni, yāni dhammaihi ārakā|

tāni āraucayissāmi, taṃ suṇātha yathā tathaṃ||

‘‘kuddhau hi pitaraṃ hanti, hanti kuddhau samātaraṃ|

kuddhau hi brāhmaṇaṃ hanti, hanti kuddhau puthuj‍janaṃ||

‘‘yāya  mātu bhatau pausau, imaṃ laukaṃ avaikkhati|

tampi pāṇadadiṃ santiṃ, hanti kuddhau puthuj‍janau||

‘‘attūpamā hi tai sattā, attā hi paramau paramaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) piyau|

hanti  kuddhau puthuttānaṃ, nānārūpaisu mucchitau||

‘‘asinā hanti attānaṃ, visaṃ khādanti mucchitā|

raj‍juyā bajjha mīyanti, pabbatāmapi kandarai||

‘‘bhūnahac‍cāni  bhūtahac‍cāni (sī॰ syā॰) kammāni, attamāraṇiyāni ca|

karauntā nāvabujjhanti karauntau nāvabujjhati (ka॰), kaudhajātau parābhavau||

‘‘itāyaṃ kaudharūpaina, mac‍cupāsau guhāsayau|

taṃ damaina samucchindai, pañ‍ñāvīriyaina diṭṭhiyā||

‘‘yathā maitaṃ aikamaitaṃ (syā॰), aikamaitaṃ (sī॰) akusalaṃ, samucchindaitha paṇḍitau|

tathaiva dhammai sikkhaitha, mā nau dummaṅkuyaṃ ahu||

‘‘vītakaudhā anāyāsā, vītalaubhā anussukā anissukā (sī॰ syā॰) tadaṭṭhakathāsu pana ‘‘anussukā’’ tvaiva dissati|

dantā kaudhaṃ pahantvāna, parinibbanti anāsavā’’ti parinibbissathanāsavāti (syā॰), parinibbiṃsu anāsavāti (ka॰)|| aikādasamaṃ|

abyākatavaggau chaṭṭhau|


tassuddānaṃ –

abyākatau purisagati, tissa sīha arakkhiyaṃ|

kimilaṃ satta pacalā, maittā bhariyā kaudhaikādasāti||


7. mahāvaggau

1. hirīauttappasuttaṃ

65. a॰ ni॰ 5.24, 168; 2.6.50 ‘‘hirauttappai, bhikkhavai, asati hirauttappavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti indriyasaṃvarau; indriyasaṃvarai asati indriyasaṃvaravipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ  hauti sīlaṃ; sīlai asati sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai asati nibbidāvirāgavipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rukkhau sākhāpalāsavipan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi phaiggupi sāraupi na pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, hirauttappai asati hirauttappavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti indriyasaṃvarau; indriyasaṃvarai asati indriyasaṃvaravipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti sīlaṃ; sīlai asati sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai asati nibbidāvirāgavipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ|

‘‘hirauttappai, bhikkhavai, sati hirauttappasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti indriyasaṃvarau; indriyasaṃvarai sati indriyasaṃvarasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti sīlaṃ; sīlai sati sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti sammāsamādhi; sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ; yathābhūtañāṇadassanai sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti nibbidāvirāgau; nibbidāvirāgai sati nibbidāvirāgasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rukkhau sākhāpalāsasampan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi phaiggupi sāraupi pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, hirauttappai sati hirauttappasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassana’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sattasūriyasuttaṃ

66. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati ambapālivanai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; adhuvā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; anassāsikā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā| yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, alamaiva sabbasaṅkhāraisu nibbindituṃ alaṃ viraj‍jituṃ alaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ|

‘‘sinairu, bhikkhavai, pabbatarājā caturāsītiyaujanasahassāni āyāmaina, caturāsītiyaujanasahassāni vitthāraina, caturāsītiyaujanasahassāni mahāsamuddai ajjhaugāḷhau, caturāsītiyaujanasahassāni mahāsamuddā ac‍cuggatau| hauti khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina bahūni vassāni bahūni vassasatāni bahūni vassasahassāni bahūni vassasatasahassāni daivau na vassati| daivai khau pana, bhikkhavai, avassantai yai kaicimai bījagāmabhūtagāmā ausadhitiṇavanappatayau tai ussussanti visussanti, na bhavanti| aivaṃ anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; aivaṃ adhuvā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ|

‘‘hauti khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina dutiyau sūriyau pātubhavati| dutiyassa, bhikkhavai, sūriyassa pātubhāvā yā kāci kun‍nadiyau kusaubbhā kussubbhau (sī॰), kussaubbhā (syā॰) tā ussussanti visussanti, na bhavanti| aivaṃ anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ|

‘‘hauti khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina tatiyau  sūriyau  pātubhavati| tatiyassa, bhikkhavai, sūriyassa pātubhāvā yā kāci mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, tā ussussanti visussanti, na bhavanti| aivaṃ anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ|

‘‘hauti khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina catutthau sūriyau pātubhavati| catutthassa, bhikkhavai, sūriyassa pātubhāvā yai tai mahāsarā yatau imā mahānadiyau pavattanti, saiyyathidaṃ – anautattā, sīhapapātā, rathakārā, kaṇṇamuṇḍā, kuṇālā, chaddantā, mandākiniyā, tā ussussanti visussanti, na bhavanti| aivaṃ anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ|

‘‘hauti khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina pañ‍camau sūriyau pātubhavati| pañ‍camassa, bhikkhavai, sūriyassa  pātubhāvā yaujanasatikānipi mahāsamuddai udakāni augacchanti, dviyaujanasatikānipi mahāsamuddai udakāni augacchanti, tiyaujanasatikānipi, catuyaujanasatikānipi, pañ‍cayaujanasatikānipi, chayaujanasatikānipi, sattayaujanasatikānipi mahāsamuddai udakāni augacchanti; sattatālampi mahāsamuddai udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, chatālampi, pañ‍catālampi, catutālampi, titālampi, dvitālampi, tālamattampi mahāsamuddai udakaṃ saṇṭhāti; sattapaurisampi mahāsamuddai udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, chapaurisampi, pañ‍capaurisampi, catupaurisampi, tipaurisampi, dvipaurisampi, paurisampi paurisamattampi (syā॰), aḍḍhapaurisampi, kaṭimattampi, jaṇṇukāmattampi, gaupphakamattampi mahāsamuddai udakaṃ saṇṭhāti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, saradasamayai thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai tattha tattha gaupadaisu gaupphakapadaisaisu (ka॰) udakāni ṭhitāni  haunti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tattha tattha gaupphakamattāni gaupadamattāni (sī॰ syā॰) mahāsamuddai udakāni ṭhitāni haunti| pañ‍camassa, bhikkhavai, sūriyassa pātubhāvā aṅgulipabbamattampi mahāsamuddai udakaṃ na hauti| aivaṃ anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ|

‘‘hauti khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina chaṭṭhau sūriyau pātubhavati| chaṭṭhassa, bhikkhavai, sūriyassa pātubhāvā ayañ‍ca mahāpathavī sinairu ca pabbatarājā dhūmāyanti saṃdhūmāyanti sampadhūmāyanti dhūpāyanti sandhūpāyanti sampadhūpāyanti (sī॰ syā॰)| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kumbhakārapākau ālaipitau ālimpitau (sī॰ syā॰) paṭhamaṃ dhūmaiti saṃdhūmaiti sampadhūmaiti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, chaṭṭhassa sūriyassa pātubhāvā  ayañ‍ca mahāpathavī sinairu ca pabbatarājā dhūmāyanti saṃdhūmāyanti sampadhūmāyanti| aivaṃ anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ|

‘‘hauti khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci dīghassa addhunau ac‍cayaina sattamau sūriyau pātubhavati| sattamassa, bhikkhavai, sūriyassa pātubhāvā ayañ‍ca mahāpathavī sinairu ca pabbatarājā ādippanti paj‍jalanti aikajālā bhavanti| imissā ca, bhikkhavai, mahāpathaviyā sinairussa  ca pabbatarājassa  jhāyamānānaṃ dayhamānānaṃ ac‍ci vātaina khittā yāva brahmalaukāpi gacchati| sinairussa, bhikkhavai, pabbatarājassa jhāyamānassa dayhamānassa vinassamānassa mahatā taijaukhandhaina abhibhūtassa yaujanasatikānipi kūṭāni paluj‍janti dviyaujanasatikānipi, tiyaujanasatikānipi, catuyaujanasatikānipi, pañ‍cayaujanasatikānipi kūṭāni paluj‍janti| imissā ca, bhikkhavai, mahāpathaviyā sinairussa ca pabbatarājassa jhāyamānānaṃ dayhamānānaṃ naiva chārikā pañ‍ñāyati na masi| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sappissa vā tailassa vā jhāyamānassa dayhamānassa naiva chārikā pañ‍ñāyati na masi; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imissā ca mahāpathaviyā sinairussa ca pabbatarājassa jhāyamānānaṃ dayhamānānaṃ naiva chārikā pañ‍ñāyati na masi| aivaṃ anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; aivaṃ adhuvā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; aivaṃ anassāsikā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā| yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, alamaiva sabbasaṅkhāraisu nibbindituṃ alaṃ viraj‍jituṃ alaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, kau mantā kau saddhātā – ‘ayañ‍ca pathavī sinairu ca pabbatarājā dayhissanti vinassissanti, na bhavissantī’ti añ‍ñatra diṭṭhapadaihi?

a॰ ni॰ 6.54; 7.73 ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sunaittau nāma satthā ahausi titthakarau kāmaisu vītarāgau| sunaittassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, satthunau anaikāni sāvakasatāni ahaisuṃ| sunaittau, bhikkhavai, satthā sāvakānaṃ brahmalaukasahabyatāya dhammaṃ daisaisi| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, sunaittassa satthunau brahmalaukasahabyatāya dhammaṃ daisaintassa sabbaina sabbaṃ sāsanaṃ ājāniṃsu tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ brahmalaukaṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu| yai na sabbaina sabbaṃ sāsanaṃ ājāniṃsu tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā appaikac‍cai paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu, appaikac‍cai nimmānaratīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu, appaikac‍cai tusitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu, appaikac‍cai yāmānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu, appaikac‍cai tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu, appaikac‍cai cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu, appaikac‍cai khattiyamahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu, appaikac‍cai brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu, appaikac‍cai gahapatimahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, sunaittassa satthunau aitadahausi – ‘na khau maitaṃ patirūpaṃ yauhaṃ sāvakānaṃ samasamagatiyau assaṃ abhisamparāyaṃ, yaṃnūnāhaṃ uttari maittaṃ uttari maggaṃ (ka॰) bhāvaiyya’’’nti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, sunaittau satthā satta vassāni maittaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaisi| satta vassāni maittaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaitvā satta saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai nayimaṃ laukaṃ punarāgamāsi| saṃvaṭṭamānai sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, laukai ābhassarūpagau hauti| vivaṭṭamānai laukai suñ‍ñaṃ brahmavimānaṃ upapaj‍jati| tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmā hauti mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī| chattiṃsakkhattuṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau ahausi daivānamindau| anaikasatakkhattuṃ rājā ahausi cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau| parausahassaṃ khau panassa puttā ahaisuṃ sūrā vīraṅgarūpā parasainappamaddanā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ sāgarapariyantaṃ adaṇḍaina asatthaina dhammaina abhivijiya ajjhāvasi| sau hi nāma, bhikkhavai, sunaittau satthā aivaṃ dīghāyukau samānau aivaṃ ciraṭṭhitikau aparimuttau ahausi – ‘jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, aparimuttau  dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘taṃ kissa haitu? catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? ariyassa, bhikkhavai, sīlassa ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā, ariyassa samādhissa ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā, ariyāya pañ‍ñāya ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā, ariyāya vimuttiyā ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyaṃ sīlaṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, ariyau samādhi anubaudhau paṭividdhau, ariyā pañ‍ñā anubaudhā paṭividdhā, ariyā vimutti anubaudhā paṭividdhā, ucchin‍nā bhavataṇhā, khīṇā bhavanaitti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti| idamavauca  bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘sīlaṃ  samādhi pañ‍ñā ca, vimutti ca anuttarā|

anubuddhā imai dhammā, gautamaina yasassinā||

‘‘iti buddhau abhiñ‍ñāya, dhammamakkhāsi bhikkhunaṃ|

dukkhassantakarau satthā, cakkhumā parinibbutau’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. nagaraupamasuttaṃ

67. ‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ sattahi nagaraparikkhāraihi suparikkhataṃ suparikkhittaṃ (ka॰) hauti, catun‍nañ‍ca āhārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ akaraṇīyaṃ bāhiraihi pac‍catthikaihi pac‍cāmittaihi|

‘‘katamaihi sattahi nagaraparikkhāraihi suparikkhataṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai aisikā hauti gambhīranaimā gambhīranaimi (ka॰) sunikhātā acalā asampavaidhī asampavaidhi (sī॰ syā॰)| iminā paṭhamaina nagaraparikkhāraina suparikkhataṃ hauti rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ  guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai parikhā hauti gambhīrā caiva vitthatā ca| iminā dutiyaina nagaraparikkhāraina suparikkhataṃ hauti rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai anupariyāyapathau hauti uc‍cau caiva vitthatau ca| iminā tatiyaina nagaraparikkhāraina suparikkhataṃ hauti rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai bahuṃ āvudhaṃ san‍nicitaṃ hauti salākañ‍caiva  jaivanikañ‍ca jaivaniyañ‍ca (sī॰ aṭṭha॰)| iminā catutthaina nagaraparikkhāraina suparikkhataṃ hauti rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai bahubalakāyau paṭivasati, saiyyathidaṃ – hatthārauhā assārauhā rathikā dhanuggahā cailakā calakā piṇḍadāyakā uggā rājaputtā pakkhandinau mahānāgā sūrā cammayaudhinau dāsakaputtā| iminā pañ‍camaina nagaraparikkhāraina suparikkhataṃ hauti rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai dauvārikau hauti paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī añ‍ñātānaṃ nivāraitā ñātānaṃ pavaisaitā| iminā  chaṭṭhaina nagaraparikkhāraina suparikkhataṃ hauti rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai pākārau hauti uc‍cau caiva vitthatau  ca vāsanalaipanasampan‍nau ca| iminā sattamaina nagaraparikkhāraina suparikkhataṃ hauti rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| imaihi  sattahi nagaraparikkhāraihi suparikkhataṃ hauti|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ āhārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai bahuṃ tiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ san‍nicitaṃ hauti abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai bahuṃ sāliyavakaṃ san‍nicitaṃ hauti abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai bahuṃ tilamuggamāsāparaṇṇaṃ san‍nicitaṃ hauti abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai bahuṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ san‍nicitaṃ hauti, saiyyathidaṃ  – sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ lauṇaṃ abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ āhārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ imaihi sattahi nagaraparikkhāraihi suparikkhataṃ hauti, imaisañ‍ca catun‍naṃ āhārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimaṃ nagaraṃ akaraṇīyaṃ bāhiraihi pac‍catthikaihi pac‍cāmittaihi| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yatau ariyasāvakau sattahi saddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, catun‍nañ‍ca jhānānaṃ  ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti  akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau akaraṇīyau mārassa akaraṇīyau pāpimatau| katamaihi sattahi saddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti?

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai aisikā hauti gambhīranaimā sunikhātā acalā asampavaidhī abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ ‘itipi sau…pai॰… buddhau bhagavā’ti| saddhaisikau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaiti; sāvaj‍jaṃ pajahati, anavaj‍jaṃ bhāvaiti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| iminā paṭhamaina saddhammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai parikkhā hauti gambhīrā caiva vitthatā ca abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau hirīmā hauti, hirīyati kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina, hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| hirīparikkhau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaiti; sāvaj‍jaṃ pajahati, anavaj‍jaṃ bhāvaiti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| iminā dutiyaina saddhammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|
