‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai anupariyāyapathau hauti uc‍cau caiva vitthatau ca abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau  auttappī hauti, auttappati kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina, auttappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| auttappapariyāyapathau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaiti; sāvaj‍jaṃ pajahati, anavaj‍jaṃ bhāvaiti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| iminā tatiyaina saddhammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai bahuṃ āvudhaṃ san‍nicitaṃ hauti salākañ‍caiva jaivanikañ‍ca abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| sutāvudhau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau akusalaṃ  pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaiti; sāvaj‍jaṃ pajahati, anavaj‍jaṃ bhāvaiti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| iminā catutthaina saddhammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai bahubalakāyau paṭivasati, saiyyathidaṃ – hatthārauhā assārauhā rathikā dhanuggahā cailakā calakā piṇḍadāyakā uggā rājaputtā pakkhandinau mahānāgā sūrā cammayaudhinau dāsakaputtā abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| vīriyabalakāyau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaiti; sāvaj‍jaṃ pajahati, anavaj‍jaṃ bhāvaiti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| iminā pañ‍camaina saddhammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai dauvārikau hauti paṇḍitau byattau maidhāvī añ‍ñātānaṃ nivāraitā ñātānaṃ pavaisaitā abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau satimā hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| satidauvārikau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaiti; sāvaj‍jaṃ pajahati, anavaj‍jaṃ bhāvaiti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| iminā chaṭṭhaina saddhammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai pākārau hauti uc‍cau caiva vitthatau ca  vāsanalaipanasampan‍nau ca abbhantarānaṃ guttiyā bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| pañ‍ñāvāsanalaipanasampan‍nau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaiti; sāvaj‍jaṃ pajahati, anavaj‍jaṃ bhāvaiti; suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| iminā sattamaina saddhammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| imaihi sattahi saddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘katamaisaṃ  catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai bahuṃ tiṇakaṭṭhaudakaṃ san‍nicitaṃ hauti abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati attanau ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya auk‍kamanāya nibbānassa|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai bahuṃ  sāliyavakaṃ san‍nicitaṃ hauti abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya  bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati attanau ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya auk‍kamanāya nibbānassa|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai bahuṃ tilamuggamāsāparaṇṇaṃ san‍nicitaṃ hauti abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati attanau ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya auk‍kamanāya nibbānassa|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau pac‍cantimai nagarai bahuṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ san‍nicitaṃ hauti, saiyyathidaṃ – sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ lauṇaṃ abbhantarānaṃ ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya bāhirānaṃ paṭighātāya| aivamaivaṃ khau bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sukhassa ca pahānā  dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati attanau ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya auk‍kamanāya nibbānassa| imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī|

‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau imaihi sattahi saddhammaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, imaisañ‍ca catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau akaraṇīyau mārassa akaraṇīyau pāpimatau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dhammañ‍ñūsuttaṃ

68. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti  …pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi sattahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammañ‍ñū ca hauti atthañ‍ñū ca attañ‍ñū ca mattañ‍ñū ca kālañ‍ñū ca parisañ‍ñū ca puggalaparauparañ‍ñū ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammañ‍ñū hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaṃ jānāti – suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthaṃ udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ| nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaṃ jānaiyya – suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ…pai॰… abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ, nayidha ‘dhammañ‍ñū’ti vuc‍caiyya| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaṃ jānāti – suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ…pai॰… abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ, tasmā ‘dhammañ‍ñū’ti vuc‍cati| iti dhammañ‍ñū|

‘‘atthañ‍ñū ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tassa tassaiva bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānāti – ‘ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau, ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau’ti| nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tassa tassaiva bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānaiyya – ‘ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau, ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau’ti, nayidha ‘atthañ‍ñū’ti vuc‍caiyya| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tassa tassaiva bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānāti – ‘ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau, ayaṃ  imassa bhāsitassa atthau’ti, tasmā ‘atthañ‍ñū’ti vuc‍cati| iti dhammañ‍ñū, atthañ‍ñū|

‘‘attañ‍ñū  ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attānaṃ jānāti – ‘aittakaumhi saddhāya sīlaina sutaina cāgaina pañ‍ñāya paṭibhānainā’ti| nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attānaṃ jānaiyya – ‘aittakaumhi saddhāya sīlaina sutaina cāgaina pañ‍ñāya paṭibhānainā’ti, nayidha ‘attañ‍ñū’ti vuc‍caiyya| yasmā ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu attānaṃ jānāti – ‘aittakaumhi saddhāya sīlaina sutaina cāgaina pañ‍ñāya paṭibhānainā’ti, tasmā ‘attañ‍ñū’ti vuc‍cati| iti dhammañ‍ñū, atthañ‍ñū, attañ‍ñū|

‘‘mattañ‍ñū  ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mattaṃ jānāti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ paṭiggahaṇāya| nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mattaṃ jānaiyya cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ paṭiggahaṇāya, nayidha ‘mattañ‍ñū’ti vuc‍caiyya| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mattaṃ jānāti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ paṭiggahaṇāya, tasmā ‘mattañ‍ñū’ti vuc‍cati| iti dhammañ‍ñū, atthañ‍ñū, attañ‍ñū, mattañ‍ñū|

‘‘kālañ‍ñū ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu, kālaṃ jānāti – ‘ayaṃ kālau uddaisassa, ayaṃ kālau paripucchāya, ayaṃ kālau yaugassa, ayaṃ kālau paṭisal‍lānassā’ti| nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaṃ jānaiyya – ‘ayaṃ kālau uddaisassa, ayaṃ kālau paripucchāya, ayaṃ kālau yaugassa, ayaṃ kālau paṭisal‍lānassā’ti, nayidha ‘kālañ‍ñū’ti vuc‍caiyya| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaṃ jānāti – ‘ayaṃ kālau uddaisassa, ayaṃ kālau paripucchāya, ayaṃ kālau yaugassa, ayaṃ kālau paṭisal‍lānassā’ti, tasmā ‘kālañ‍ñū’ti vuc‍cati| iti dhammañ‍ñū, atthañ‍ñū, attañ‍ñū, mattañ‍ñū, kālañ‍ñū|

‘‘parisañ‍ñū ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parisaṃ jānāti – ‘ayaṃ khattiyaparisā, ayaṃ brāhmaṇaparisā, ayaṃ gahapatiparisā, ayaṃ samaṇaparisā| tattha aivaṃ upasaṅkamitabbaṃ, aivaṃ  ṭhātabbaṃ, aivaṃ kattabbaṃ, aivaṃ nisīditabbaṃ, aivaṃ bhāsitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tuṇhī bhavitabba’nti| nau cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parisaṃ jānaiyya – ‘ayaṃ khattiyaparisā…pai॰… aivaṃ tuṇhī bhavitabba’nti, nayidha ‘parisañ‍ñū’ti vuc‍caiyya| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parisaṃ jānāti – ‘ayaṃ khattiyaparisā, ayaṃ brāhmaṇaparisā, ayaṃ gahapatiparisā, ayaṃ samaṇaparisā| tattha aivaṃ upasaṅkamitabbaṃ, aivaṃ ṭhātabbaṃ, aivaṃ kattabbaṃ, aivaṃ nisīditabbaṃ, aivaṃ bhāsitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tuṇhī bhavitabba’nti, tasmā ‘parisañ‍ñū’ti vuc‍cati| iti dhammañ‍ñū, atthañ‍ñū, attañ‍ñū, mattañ‍ñū, kālañ‍ñū, parisañ‍ñū|

‘‘puggalaparauparañ‍ñū ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sabbatthapi idha ārambhai ‘‘bhikkhunau’’ tvaiva dissati dvayaina puggalā viditā haunti| dvai puggalā – aikau ariyānaṃ dassanakāmau, aikau ariyānaṃ na dassanakāmau| yvāyaṃ puggalau ariyānaṃ na dassanakāmau, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| yvāyaṃ puggalau ariyānaṃ dassanakāmau, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘dvai puggalā ariyānaṃ dassanakāmā – aikau saddhammaṃ sautukāmau, aikau saddhammaṃ na sautukāmau| yvāyaṃ puggalau saddhammaṃ na sautukāmau, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| yvāyaṃ puggalau saddhammaṃ sautukāmau, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘dvai puggalā saddhammaṃ sautukāmā – aikau auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti, aikau anauhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti| yvāyaṃ puggalau anauhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| yvāyaṃ puggalau auhitasautau  dhammaṃ suṇāti, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘dvai puggalā auhitasautā dhammaṃ suṇanti suṇantā (ka॰) – aikau sutvā dhammaṃ dhāraiti, aikau sutvā dhammaṃ na dhāraiti| yvāyaṃ puggalau sutvā na dhammaṃ  dhāraiti, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| yvāyaṃ puggalau sutvā dhammaṃ dhāraiti, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘dvai puggalā sutvā dhammaṃ dhārainti dhāraintā (ka॰) – aikau dhātānaṃ dhatānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati, aikau dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ na upaparikkhati| yvāyaṃ puggalau dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ na upaparikkhati, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| yvāyaṃ puggalau dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘dvai puggalā dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhanti upaparikkhantā (ka॰) – aikau atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau, aikau atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya na dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau| yvāyaṃ puggalau atthamañ‍ñāya  dhammamañ‍ñāya na dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| yvāyaṃ puggalau atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau|

‘‘dvai puggalā atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā – aikau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya, aikau attahitāya ca paṭipan‍nau  parahitāya ca| yvāyaṃ puggalau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina gārayhau| yvāyaṃ puggalau atthahitāya ca paṭipan‍nau parahitāya ca, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina pāsaṃsau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhikkhunā (sī॰ syā॰) dvayaina puggalā viditā haunti| aivaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu puggalaparauparañ‍ñū hauti| ‘‘imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau  bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. pāricchattakasuttaṃ

69. ‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau paṇḍupalāsau hauti, attamanā, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā tasmiṃ samayai haunti – ‘paṇḍupalāsau dāni pāricchattakau kauviḷārau nacirassaiva dāni pan‍napalāsau bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau pan‍napalāsau hauti, attamanā, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā tasmiṃ samayai haunti – ‘pan‍napalāsau dāni pāricchattakau kauviḷārau nacirassaiva dāni jālakajātau bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau jālakajātau hauti, attamanā, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā tasmiṃ samayai haunti – ‘jālakajātau dāni pāricchattakau kauviḷārau nacirassaiva dāni khārakajātau bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau khārakajātau hauti, attamanā, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā tasmiṃ samayai haunti – ‘khārakajātau dāni pāricchattakau kauviḷārau nacirassaiva dāni kuṭumalakajātau kuḍumalakajātau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau kuṭumalakajātau hauti, attamanā, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā  tasmiṃ samayai haunti – ‘kuṭumalakajātau dāni pāricchattakau kauviḷārau nacirassaiva dāni kaurakajātau  kaukāsakajātau (sī॰ syā॰), kausakajātau (ka॰) bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau kaurakajātau hauti, attamanā, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā tasmiṃ samayai haunti – ‘kaurakajātau dāni pāricchattakau kauviḷārau nacirassaiva dāni sabbaphāliphul‍lau sabbapāliphul‍lau (sī॰ pī॰) bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau sabbaphāliphul‍lau hauti, attamanā, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā pāricchattakassa kauviḷārassa mūlai dibbai cattārau māsai pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitā samaṅgībhūtā paricārainti|

‘‘sabbaphāliphul‍lassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, pāricchattakassa kauviḷārassa samantā pañ‍ñāsayaujanāni ābhāya phuṭaṃ hauti, anuvātaṃ yaujanasataṃ gandhau gacchati, ayamānubhāvau pāricchattakassa kauviḷārassa|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ samayai ariyasāvakau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāya caitaiti, paṇḍupalāsau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau tasmiṃ samayai hauti daivānaṃva tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, pan‍napalāsau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau tasmiṃ samayai hauti daivānaṃva tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, jālakajātau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau tasmiṃ samayai hauti daivānaṃva tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, khārakajātau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau tasmiṃ samayai hauti  daivānaṃva tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai  ariyasāvakau pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, kuṭumalakajātau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau tasmiṃ samayai hauti daivānaṃva tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, kaurakajātau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau tasmiṃ samayai hauti daivānaṃva tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati, sabbaphāliphul‍lau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau tasmiṃ samayai hauti daivānaṃva tāvatiṃsānaṃ pāricchattakau kauviḷārau|

‘‘tasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai bhummā daivā saddamanussāvainti – ‘aisau itthan‍nāmau āyasmā itthan‍nāmassa āyasmatau saddhivihārikau amukamhā gāmā vā nigamā vā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’ti| bhummānaṃ daivānaṃ saddaṃ sutvā cātumahārājikā daivā…pai॰… tāvatiṃsā daivā… yāmā daivā… tusitā daivā… nimmānaratī daivā… paranimmitavasavattī daivā… brahmakāyikā daivā saddamanussāvainti – ‘aisau itthan‍nāmau āyasmā itthan‍nāmassa āyasmatau saddhivihārikau amukamhā gāmā vā nigamā vā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’ti| itiha taina  khaṇaina taina muhuttaina yāva brahmalaukā saddau sādhukārasaddau (sī॰ aṭṭha॰, ka॰ aṭṭha॰) abbhuggacchati, ayamānubhāvau khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sak‍kac‍casuttaṃ

70. atha  khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhikkhu, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharantau akusalaṃ pajahaiyya, kusalaṃ bhāvaiyyā’’ti? atha khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘satthāraṃ khau, bhikkhu, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharantau akusalaṃ pajahaiyya, kusalaṃ bhāvaiyya| dhammaṃ khau, bhikkhu…pai॰… saṅghaṃ khau, bhikkhu…pai॰… sikkhaṃ khau, bhikkhu…pai॰… samādhiṃ khau, bhikkhu…pai॰… appamādaṃ khau, bhikkhu…pai॰… paṭisanthāraṃ khau, bhikkhu sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharantau akusalaṃ pajahaiyya, kusalaṃ bhāvaiyyā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘imai khau mai dhammā parisuddhā pariyaudātā, yaṃnūnāhaṃ imai dhammai gantvā gahaitvā (ka॰) bhagavatau āraucaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ mai imai dhammā parisuddhā caiva bhavissanti parisuddhasaṅkhātatarā ca| saiyyathāpi nāma purisau suvaṇṇanikkhaṃ adhigacchaiyya parisuddhaṃ pariyaudātaṃ| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ khau mai suvaṇṇanikkhau parisuddhau pariyaudātau, yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaṃ suvaṇṇanikkhaṃ gantvā gahaitvā (ka॰) kammārānaṃ dassaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ mai ayaṃ suvaṇṇanikkhau sakammāragatau parisuddhau caiva bhavissati parisuddhasaṅkhātatarau ca| aivamaivaṃ khau (ka॰) mai imai dhammā parisuddhā pariyaudātā, yaṃnūnāhaṃ imai dhammai gantvā gahaitvā (ka॰) bhagavatau āraucaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ mai imai dhammā parisuddhā caiva bhavissanti parisuddhasaṅkhātatarā cā’’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā  sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina  bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhikkhu, sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharantau akusalaṃ pajahaiyya, kusalaṃ bhāvaiyyā’ti? atha khau tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘satthāraṃ khau, bhikkhu, sak‍katvā garuṃ  katvā upanissāya viharantau akusalaṃ pajahaiyya, kusalaṃ bhāvaiyya| dhammaṃ khau, bhikkhu  …pai॰… paṭisanthāraṃ khau, bhikkhu sak‍katvā…pai॰… kusalaṃ bhāvaiyyā’ti| atha khau tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘imai khau mai dhammā parisuddhā pariyaudātā, yaṃnūnāhaṃ imai dhammai gantvā bhagavatau āraucaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ mai imai dhammā parisuddhā caiva bhavissanti parisuddhasaṅkhātatarā ca| saiyyathāpi nāma purisau suvaṇṇanikkhaṃ adhigacchaiyya parisuddhaṃ pariyaudātaṃ| tassa aivamassa – ayaṃ khau mai suvaṇṇanikkhau parisuddhau pariyaudātau, yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaṃ suvaṇṇanikkhaṃ gantvā kammārānaṃ dassaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ mai ayaṃ suvaṇṇanikkhau sakammāragatau parisuddhau caiva bhavissati parisuddhasaṅkhātatarau ca| aivamaivaṃ mai imai dhammā parisuddhā pariyaudātā, yaṃnūnāhaṃ imai dhammai gantvā bhagavatau āraucaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ mai imai dhammā parisuddhā caiva bhavissanti parisuddhasaṅkhātatarā  cā’’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! satthāraṃ khau, sāriputta, bhikkhu sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharantau akusalaṃ pajahaiyya, kusalaṃ bhāvaiyya| dhammaṃ khau, sāriputta, bhikkhu sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharantau akusalaṃ pajahaiyya, kusalaṃ bhāvaiyya| saṅghaṃ khau…pai॰… sikkhaṃ khau… samādhiṃ khau… appamādaṃ khau… paṭisanthāraṃ khau, sāriputta, bhikkhu sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharantau akusalaṃ pajahaiyya, kusalaṃ bhāvaiyyā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imassa khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| sau vata, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai sagāravau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammaipi sau agāravau’’|

‘‘sau vata, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai agāravau saṅghai sagāravau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai agāravau saṅghaipi sau agāravau|

‘‘sau vata, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai agāravau saṅghai agāravau sikkhāya sagāravau  bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai agāravau saṅghai agāravau sikkhāyapi sau agāravau|

‘‘sau  vata, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai agāravau saṅghai agāravau  sikkhāya agāravau samādhismiṃ sagāravau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai agāravau saṅghai agāravau sikkhāya agāravau samādhismimpi sau agāravau|

‘‘sau vata, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai agāravau saṅghai agāravau sikkhāya agāravau samādhismiṃ agāravau appamādai sagāravau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai agāravau saṅghai agāravau sikkhāya agāravau samādhismiṃ  agāravau appamādaipi sau agāravau|

‘‘sau vata, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai agāravau saṅghai agāravau sikkhāya agāravau samādhismiṃ agāravau appamādai agāravau paṭisanthārai sagāravau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari agāravau…pai॰… appamādai agāravau paṭisanthāraipi sau agāravau|

‘‘sau vata, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau dhammai agāravau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau dhammaipi sau sagāravau…pai॰…|

‘‘sau vata, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau…pai॰  … appamādai sagāravau paṭisanthārai agāravau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau…pai॰…  appamādai sagāravau paṭisanthāraipi sau sagāravau|

‘‘sau vata, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau dhammaipi sagāravau bhavissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau dhammaipi sau sagāravau…pai॰…|

‘‘sau  vata, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau…pai॰… appamādai sagāravau paṭisanthāraipi sagāravau bhavissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau dhammai sagāravau saṅghai sagāravau sikkhāya sagāravau samādhismiṃ sagāravau appamādai sagāravau paṭisanthāraipi sau sagāravau’’ti|

‘‘imassa  khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! sādhu khau tvaṃ, sāriputta, imassa mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| sau vata, sāriputta, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai sagāravau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati…pai॰… yau sau, sāriputta, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai agāravau saṅghai agāravau sikkhāya agāravau samādhismiṃ agāravau appamādaipi sau agāravau|

‘‘sau vata, sāriputta, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai agāravau saṅghai agāravau sikkhāya agāravau samādhismiṃ agāravau appamādai agāravau paṭisanthārai sagāravau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, sāriputta, bhikkhu satthari agāravau dhammai agāravau saṅghai agāravau sikkhāya  agāravau samādhismiṃ agāravau appamādai agāravau paṭisanthāraipi sau agāravau|

‘‘sau vata, sāriputta, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau dhammai agāravau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati…pai॰… yau sau, sāriputta, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau dhammaipi sau sagāravau  …pai॰…|

‘‘sau vata, sāriputta, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau dhammai sagāravau…pai॰… appamādai sagāravau paṭisanthārai agāravau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, sāriputta, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau…pai॰… appamādai sagāravau paṭisanthāraipi sau sagāravau|

‘‘sau  vata, sāriputta, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau dhammaipi sagāravau bhavissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, sāriputta, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau dhammaipi sau sagāravau…pai॰…|

‘‘sau vata, sāriputta, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau…pai॰… appamādai sagāravau paṭisanthāraipi sau sagāravau bhavissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| yau sau, sāriputta, bhikkhu satthari sagāravau…pai॰… appamādai sagāravau paṭisanthāraipi sau sagāravau’’ti|

‘‘imassa  khau, sāriputta, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. bhāvanāsuttaṃ

71. ‘‘bhāvanaṃ ananuyuttassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau viharatau kiñ‍cāpi aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata mai anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍caiyyā’ti, atha khvassa naiva anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘abhāvitattā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| kissa abhāvitattā? catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ, catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ, pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ, sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ, ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭiyā aṇḍāni aṭṭha vā dasa vā dvādasa vā| tānassu kuk‍kuṭiyā na sammā adhisayitāni, na sammā parisaiditāni, na sammā paribhāvitāni| kiñ‍cāpi tassā kuk‍kuṭiyā  aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata mai kuk‍kuṭapautakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jaiyyu’nti, atha khau abhabbāva tai kuk‍kuṭapautakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jituṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭiyā aṇḍāni na sammā adhisayitāni, na sammā parisaiditāni, na sammā paribhāvitāni| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhāvanaṃ ananuyuttassa bhikkhunau viharatau kiñ‍cāpi aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata mai anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍caiyyā’ti, atha khvassa naiva anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘abhāvitattā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| kissa  abhāvitattā? catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ, catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ, pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ, sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ, ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa|

‘‘bhāvanaṃ anuyuttassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau viharatau kiñ‍cāpi na aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata mai anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍caiyyā’ti, atha khvassa anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘bhāvitattā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| kissa bhāvitattā? catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ, catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ, catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ, pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ, sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ, ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭiyā aṇḍāni aṭṭha vā dasa vā dvādasa vā| tānassu kuk‍kuṭiyā sammā adhisayitāni, sammā parisaiditāni, sammā paribhāvitāni| kiñ‍cāpi tassā kuk‍kuṭiyā na aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata mai kuk‍kuṭapautakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jaiyyu’nti, atha khau bhabbāva tai kuk‍kuṭapautakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jituṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭiyā aṇḍāni sammā adhisayitāni, sammā parisaiditāni, sammā paribhāvitāni| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhāvanaṃ anuyuttassa bhikkhunau viharatau kiñ‍cāpi na aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata  mai anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍caiyyā’ti, atha khvassa anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘bhāvitattā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| kissa bhāvitattā? catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ…pai॰… ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, phalagaṇḍassa palagaṇḍassa (?) vā phalagaṇḍantaivāsikassa vā dissantaiva khīyantai (ka॰) vāsijaṭai dissanti aṅgulipadāni (sī॰), aṅgulapadāni dissanti aṅgulapadaṃ (ka॰) aṅgulipadāni dissati dissanti (syā॰) aṅguṭṭhapadaṃ dissanti aṅgulipadāni (sī॰), aṅgulapadāni dissanti aṅgulapadaṃ (ka॰)| nau ca khvassa aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘aittakaṃ mai aj‍ja vāsijaṭassa khīṇaṃ, aittakaṃ hiyyau, aittakaṃ parai’ti, atha khvassa khīṇai ‘khīṇa’ntaiva ñāṇaṃ hauti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhāvanaṃ anuyuttassa bhikkhunau viharatau kiñ‍cāpi na aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘aittakaṃ mai aj‍ja āsavānaṃ khīṇaṃ, aittakaṃ hiyyau, aittakaṃ parai’ti, atha khvassa khīṇai ‘khīṇa’ntaiva ñāṇaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sāmuddikāya nāvāya vaittabandhanabaddhāya vaittabandhāya (ka॰) cha māsāni udakai pariyādāya haimantikaina thalai ukkhittāya vātātapaparaitāni  bandhanāni, tāni pāvussakaina maighaina abhippavuṭṭhāni appakasirainaiva parihāyanti paṭippassambhanti (sī॰ syā॰), pūtikāni bhavanti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhāvanaṃ anuyuttassa bhikkhunau viharatau appakasirainaiva saṃyaujanāni paṭippassambhanti, pūtikāni bhavantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. aggikkhandhaupamasuttaṃ

72. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ carati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ| addasā khau bhagavā addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ padaisai mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ sañ‍jautibhūtaṃ (syā॰)| disvāna maggā auk‍kamma añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, amuṃ mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūta’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau varaṃ – yaṃ amuṃ mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ āliṅgaitvā upanisīdaiyya vā upanipaj‍jaiyya vā, yaṃ vā khattiyakañ‍ñaṃ vā brāhmaṇakañ‍ñaṃ vā gahapatikañ‍ñaṃ vā mudutalunahatthapādaṃ āliṅgaitvā upanisīdaiyya vā upanipaj‍jaiyya vā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, varaṃ – yaṃ khattiyakañ‍ñaṃ vā brāhmaṇakañ‍ñaṃ vā gahapatikañ‍ñaṃ vā mudutalunahatthapādaṃ āliṅgaitvā upanisīdaiyya vā upanipaj‍jaiyya vā, dukkhañhaitaṃ, bhantai, yaṃ amuṃ mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ āliṅgaitvā upanisīdaiyya vā upanipaj‍jaiyya vā’’ti|

‘‘āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi  vau, bhikkhavai, yathā aitadaiva tassa varaṃ dussīlassa pāpadhammassa asucisaṅkassarasamācārassa paṭicchan‍nakammantassa assamaṇassa samaṇapaṭiñ‍ñassa abrahmacārissa brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñassa antaupūtikassa avassutassa kasambujātassa yaṃ amuṃ mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ āliṅgaitvā upanisīdaiyya vā upanipaj‍jaiyya vā| taṃ kissa haitu? tataunidānañhi sau, bhikkhavai, maraṇaṃ  vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ  vā dukkhaṃ, na tvaiva tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya|

‘‘yañ‍ca khau sau, bhikkhavai, dussīlau pāpadhammau asucisaṅkassarasamācārau…pai॰… kasambujātau khattiyakañ‍ñaṃ vā brāhmaṇakañ‍ñaṃ vā gahapatikañ‍ñaṃ vā mudutalunahatthapādaṃ āliṅgaitvā upanisīdati vā upanipaj‍jati vā, tañhi tassa taṃ hissa (ka॰), bhikkhavai, hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau varaṃ – yaṃ balavā purisau daḷhāya vālaraj‍juyā ubhau jaṅghā vaiṭhaitvā ghaṃsaiyya – sā chaviṃ chindaiyya chaviṃ chaitvā cammaṃ chindaiyya cammaṃ chaitvā maṃsaṃ chindaiyya maṃsaṃ chaitvā nhāruṃ chindaiyya nhāruṃ chaitvā aṭṭhiṃ chindaiyya aṭṭhiṃ chaitvā aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhaiyya, yaṃ vā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā abhivādanaṃ sādiyaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, varaṃ – yaṃ khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā abhivādanaṃ sādiyaiyya, dukkhañhaitaṃ, bhantai, yaṃ balavā purisau daḷhāya vālaraj‍juyā…pai॰… aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, yathā aitadaiva tassa varaṃ dussīlassa…pai॰… kasambujātassa yaṃ balavā purisau daḷhāya vālaraj‍juyā ubhau jaṅghā vaiṭhaitvā…pai॰… aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tataunidānañhi sau, bhikkhavai, maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tvaiva tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| yañ‍ca  khau sau, bhikkhavai, dussīlau…pai॰… kasambujātau khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā abhivādanaṃ sādiyati, tañhi tassa, bhikkhavai, hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau varaṃ – yaṃ balavā purisau tiṇhāya sattiyā tailadhautāya pac‍caurasmiṃ paharaiyya, yaṃ vā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā añ‍jalikammaṃ sādiyaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, varaṃ – yaṃ khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā  gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā añ‍jalikammaṃ sādiyaiyya, dukkhañhaitaṃ, bhantai, yaṃ balavā purisau tiṇhāya sattiyā  tailadhautāya pac‍caurasmiṃ paharaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, yathā aitadaiva tassa varaṃ dussīlassa…pai॰… kasambujātassa yaṃ balavā purisau tiṇhāya sattiyā tailadhautāya pac‍caurasmiṃ paharaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tataunidānañhi sau, bhikkhavai, maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tvaiva tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ  nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| yañ‍ca khau sau, bhikkhavai, dussīlau pāpadhammau…pai॰… kasambujātau khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā añ‍jalikammaṃ sādiyati, tañhi tassa, bhikkhavai, hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau varaṃ – yaṃ balavā purisau tattaina ayaupaṭṭaina ādittaina sampaj‍jalitaina  sajautibhūtaina kāyaṃ sampalivaiṭhaiyya, yaṃ vā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādaiyyaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, varaṃ – yaṃ khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā…pai॰… saddhādaiyyaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyya, dukkhañhaitaṃ, bhantai, yaṃ balavā purisau tattaina ayaupaṭṭaina ādittaina sampaj‍jalitaina sajautibhūtaina kāyaṃ sampalivaiṭhaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, yathā aitadaiva tassa varaṃ dussīlassa…pai॰… kasambujātassa yaṃ balavā purisau tattaina ayaupaṭṭaina ādittaina  sampaj‍jalitaina sajautibhūtaina kāyaṃ sampalivaiṭhaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tataunidānañhi sau, bhikkhavai, maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tvaiva tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| yañ‍ca khau sau, bhikkhavai, dussīlau…pai॰… kasambujātau khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādaiyyaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jati, tañhi tassa, bhikkhavai, hauti dīgharattaṃ  ahitāya dukkhāya kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau varaṃ – yaṃ balavā purisau tattaina ayausaṅkunā mukhaṃ vivaritvā tattaṃ lauhaguḷaṃ ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ mukhai pakkhipaiyya – taṃ tassa auṭṭhampi dahaiyya ḍahaiyya (katthaci) mukhampi dahaiyya jivhampi dahaiyya kaṇṭhampi dahaiyya urampi ma॰ ni॰ 3.270 daivadūtasuttai pana ‘‘udarampi’’ iti vidaisapāṭhau dissati  dahaiyya antampi antaguṇampi ādāya adhaubhāgā adhaubhāgaṃ (ka॰) nikkhamaiyya, yaṃ vā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādaiyyaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ  paribhuñ‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, varaṃ – yaṃ khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādaiyyaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyya, dukkhañhaitaṃ, bhantai, yaṃ balavā purisau tattaina ayausaṅkunā mukhaṃ vivaritvā tattaṃ lauhaguḷaṃ ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ mukhai pakkhipaiyya – taṃ tassa auṭṭhampi dahaiyya mukhampi dahaiyya jivhampi  dahaiyya kaṇṭhampi dahaiyya urampi dahaiyya antampi antaguṇampi ādāya adhaubhāgaṃ nikkhamaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, yathā aitadaiva tassa varaṃ dussīlassa…pai॰… kasambujātassa yaṃ balavā purisau tattaina ayausaṅkunā mukhaṃ vivaritvā tattaṃ lauhaguḷaṃ ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ mukhai pakkhipaiyya – taṃ tassa auṭṭhampi dahaiyya mukhampi dahaiyya jivhampi dahaiyya kaṇṭhampi dahaiyya urampi dahaiyya antampi antaguṇampi ādāya adhaubhāgaṃ nikkhamaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tataunidānañhi sau, bhikkhavai, maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tvaiva tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| yañ‍ca khau sau, bhikkhavai, dussīlau pāpadhammau…pai॰… kasambujātau khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādaiyyaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jati, tañhi tassa hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau varaṃ – yaṃ balavā purisau sīsai vā gahaitvā khandhai vā gahaitvā  tattaṃ ayaumañ‍caṃ vā ayaupīṭhaṃ vā abhinisīdāpaiyya vā abhinipaj‍jāpaiyya vā, yaṃ vā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ  vā saddhādaiyyaṃ mañ‍capīṭhaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā (ka॰) paribhuj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, varaṃ – yaṃ khattiyamahāsālānaṃ  vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādaiyyaṃ mañ‍capīṭhaṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyya, dukkhañhaitaṃ, bhantai, yaṃ balavā purisau sīsai vā gahaitvā khandhai vā gahaitvā tattaṃ ayaumañ‍caṃ vā ayaupīṭhaṃ vā abhinisīdāpaiyya vā abhinipaj‍jāpaiyya vā’’ti|

‘‘āraucayāmi  vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, yathā aitadaiva tassa varaṃ dussīlassa…pai॰… kasambujātassa yaṃ balavā purisau sīsai vā gahaitvā khandhai vā gahaitvā tattaṃ ayaumañ‍caṃ vā ayaupīṭhaṃ vā abhinisīdāpaiyya vā abhinipaj‍jāpaiyya vā| taṃ kissa haitu? tataunidānañhi sau, bhikkhavai, maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tvaiva tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| yañ‍ca khau sau, bhikkhavai, dussīlau pāpadhammau…pai॰… kasambujātau khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādaiyyaṃ mañ‍capīṭhaṃ paribhuñ‍jati| tañhi tassa, bhikkhavai, hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau varaṃ – yaṃ balavā purisau uddhaṃpādaṃ adhausiraṃ gahaitvā tattāya lauhakumbhiyā pakkhipaiyya ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya – sau tattha phaiṇuddaihakaṃ pac‍camānau sakimpi uddhaṃ gacchaiyya sakimpi adhau gacchaiyya sakimpi tiriyaṃ gacchaiyya, yaṃ vā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā  brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādaiyyaṃ vihāraṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, varaṃ – yaṃ khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā  saddhādaiyyaṃ vihāraṃ paribhuñ‍jaiyya, dukkhañhaitaṃ, bhantai, yaṃ balavā purisau uddhaṃpādaṃ adhausiraṃ gahaitvā tattāya lauhakumbhiyā pakkhipaiyya ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya – sau tattha phaiṇuddaihakaṃ pac‍camānau sakimpi uddhaṃ gacchaiyya sakimpi adhau gacchaiyya sakimpi tiriyaṃ gacchaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, yathā aitadaiva tassa varaṃ dussīlassa pāpadhammassa…pai॰… kasambujātassa yaṃ balavā purisau uddhaṃpādaṃ adhausiraṃ gahaitvā…pai॰… sakimpi tiriyaṃ gacchaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tataunidānañhi sau, bhikkhavai, maraṇaṃ  vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ, na tvaiva tappac‍cayā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| yañ‍ca khau sau, bhikkhavai, dussīlau pāpadhammau…pai॰… kasambujātau khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā saddhādaiyyaṃ vihāraṃ paribhuñ‍jati| tañhi tassa, bhikkhavai, hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaisañ‍ca yaisaṃ (?) mayaṃ paribhuñ‍jāma cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ … parikkhārānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) taisaṃ tai kārā mahapphalā  bhavissanti mahānisaṃsā, amhākañ‍caivāyaṃ pabbaj‍jā avañjhā bhavissati saphalā saudrayā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘attatthaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, sampassamānaina alamaiva appamādaina sampādaituṃ; paratthaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, sampassamānaina  alamaiva appamādaina sampādaituṃ; ubhayatthaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, sampassamānaina alamaiva appamādaina sampādaitu’’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā idamavauca bhagavā…pai॰… (ka॰)| imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai saṭṭhimattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ uṇhaṃ lauhitaṃ mukhatau uggañchi uggacchi (ka॰)| saṭṭhimattā bhikkhū sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattiṃsu – ‘‘suduk‍karaṃ bhagavā, suduk‍karaṃ bhagavā’’ti| saṭṭhimattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsūti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sunaittasuttaṃ

73. a॰ ni॰ 6.54; 7.66 ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sunaittau nāma satthā ahausi titthakarau kāmaisu vītarāgau| sunaittassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, satthunau anaikāni sāvakasatāni ahaisuṃ| sunaittau satthā sāvakānaṃ brahmalaukasahabyatāya dhammaṃ daisaisi| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai yai khau bhikkhavai (sī॰ syā॰), sunaittassa satthunau brahmalaukasahabyatāya dhammaṃ daisaintassa cittāni nappasādaisuṃ tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, sunaittassa satthunau brahmalaukasahabyatāya dhammaṃ daisaintassa cittāni pasādaisuṃ tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, mūgapakkhau nāma satthā ahausi…pai॰… aranaimi nāma satthā ahausi…pai॰… kuddālakau kuddālau (sī॰ syā॰) nāma satthā  ahausi…pai॰… hatthipālau nāma satthā ahausi…pai॰… jautipālau nāma satthā ahausi…pai॰… arakau nāma satthā ahausi titthakarau kāmaisu vītarāgau| arakassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, satthunau anaikāni sāvakasatāni ahaisuṃ| arakau nāma satthā sāvakānaṃ brahmalaukasahabyatāya dhammaṃ daisaisi| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, arakassa satthunau brahmalaukasahabyatāya  dhammaṃ daisaintassa cittāni nappasādaisuṃ, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu| yai khau pana, bhikkhavai, arakassa satthunau brahmalaukasahabyatāya dhammaṃ daisaintassa cittāni pasādaisuṃ, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, yau imai satta satthārai titthakarai kāmaisu vītarāgai anaikasataparivārai sasāvakasaṅghai paduṭṭhacittau ak‍kausaiyya paribhāsaiyya, bahuṃ sau apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, imai satta satthārai titthakarai kāmaisu vītarāgai anaikasataparivārai sasāvakasaṅghai paduṭṭhacittau ak‍kausaiyya paribhāsaiyya, bahuṃ sau apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavaiyya| yau aikaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍naṃ puggalaṃ paduṭṭhacittau ak‍kausati paribhāsati, ayaṃ tatau bahutaraṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| taṃ kissa haitu? nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, itau bahiddhā aivarūpiṃ khantiṃ vadāmi yathāmaṃ sabrahmacārīsu’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na nau sabrahmacārīsu na tvaiva amhaṃ sabrahmacārīsu (syā॰) aṅguttaranikāyai añ‍ñathā dissati cittāni paduṭṭhāni bhavissantī’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. arakasuttaṃ

74. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, arakau nāma satthā ahausi titthakarau kāmaisu  vītarāgau| arakassa khau pana, bhikkhavai, satthunau anaikāni sāvakasatāni ahaisuṃ| arakau satthā sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – appakaṃ, brāhmaṇa, jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ lahusaṃ (ṭīkā) bahudukkhaṃ bahupāyāsaṃ mantāyaṃ mantāya (sabbattha) ṭīkā passitabbā bauddhabbaṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, tiṇaggai ussāvabindu sūriyai uggacchantai khippaṃyaiva paṭivigacchati, na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, ussāvabindūpamaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ bahudukkhaṃ bahupāyāsaṃ mantāyaṃ bauddhabbaṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai udakabubbuḷaṃ udakapupphuḷaṃ (ka॰) khippaṃyaiva paṭivigacchati, na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, udakabubbuḷūpamaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ bahudukkhaṃ bahupāyāsaṃ mantāyaṃ bauddhabbaṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, udakai daṇḍarāji khippaṃyaiva paṭivigacchati, na ciraṭṭhitikā hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, udakai daṇḍarājūpamaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ…pai॰… natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, nadī pabbataiyyā dūraṅgamā sīghasautā hārahārinī, natthi sau khaṇau vā layau vā muhuttau vā yaṃ sā yāya (ka॰) āvattati tharati (sī॰), dharati (syā॰), avatiṭṭhaiyya (?), atha khau sā gacchataiva vattataiva sandataiva; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, nadīpabbataiyyūpamaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ…pai॰… natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, balavā purisau  jivhaggai khaiḷapiṇḍaṃ saṃyūhitvā akasirainaiva vamaiyya patāpaiyya (ka॰); aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, khaiḷapiṇḍūpamaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ…pai॰… natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, divasaṃsantattai ayaukaṭāhai  maṃsapaisi maṃsapaisī (sī॰ syā॰) pakkhittā khippaṃyaiva paṭivigacchati, na ciraṭṭhitikā hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, maṃsapaisūpamaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ…pai॰… natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, gāvī vajjhā āghātanaṃ nīyamānā yaṃ yadaiva pādaṃ uddharati, santikaiva  hauti vadhassa santikaiva maraṇassa; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, gauvajjhūpamaṃ gāvīvajjhūpamaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ bahudukkhaṃ bahupāyāsaṃ  mantāyaṃ bauddhabbaṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇa’’nti|

‘‘taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina manussānaṃ saṭṭhivassasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ ahausi, pañ‍cavassasatikā kumārikā alaṃpataiyyā ahausi| taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina manussānaṃ chaḷaiva ābādhā ahaisuṃ – sītaṃ, uṇhaṃ, jighacchā, pipāsā, uc‍cārau, passāvau| sau hi nāma, bhikkhavai, arakau satthā aivaṃ dīghāyukaisu manussaisu aivaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaisu aivaṃ appābādhaisu sāvakānaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissati – ‘appakaṃ, brāhmaṇa, jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ bahudukkhaṃ bahupāyāsaṃ mantāyaṃ bauddhabbaṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇa’’’nti|

‘‘aitarahi taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘appakaṃ jīvitaṃ manussānaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ bahudukkhaṃ bahupāyāsaṃ mantāyaṃ bauddhabbaṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇa’nti| aitarahi, bhikkhavai, yau ciraṃ jīvati sau vassasataṃ appaṃ vā bhiyyau| vassasataṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, jīvantau tīṇiyaiva utusatāni jīvati – utusataṃ haimantānaṃ, utusataṃ gimhānaṃ, utusataṃ vassānaṃ| tīṇi khau pana, bhikkhavai, utusatāni jīvantau dvādasa dvādasaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) yaiva māsasatāni jīvati – cattāri māsasatāni haimantānaṃ, cattāri māsasatāni gimhānaṃ, cattāri māsasatāni vassānaṃ| dvādasa khau pana, bhikkhavai, māsasatāni jīvantau catuvīsatiyaiva addhamāsasatāni jīvati – aṭṭhaddhamāsasatāni haimantānaṃ, aṭṭhaddhamāsasatāni gimhānaṃ, aṭṭhaddhamāsasatāni vassānaṃ| catuvīsati khau pana, bhikkhavai, addhamāsasatāni jīvantau chattiṃsaṃyaiva rattisahassāni jīvati – dvādasa rattisahassāni haimantānaṃ, dvādasa rattisahassāni gimhānaṃ, dvādasa rattisahassāni vassānaṃ| chattiṃsaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, rattisahassāni jīvantau dvaisattatiyaiva dvaisattatiñ‍ñaiva (syā॰), dvaisattatiñ‍caiva (ka॰) bhattasahassāni bhuñ‍jati – catuvīsati bhattasahassāni haimantānaṃ, catuvīsati bhattasahassāni gimhānaṃ, catuvīsati bhattasahassāni vassānaṃ saddhiṃ mātuthañ‍ñāya saddhiṃ bhattantarāyaina|

‘‘tatrimai bhattantarāyā kapimiddhaupi bhattaṃ na bhuñ‍jati, dukkhitaupi bhattaṃ na bhuñ‍jati, byādhitaupi  bhattaṃ na bhuñ‍jati, upausathikaupi bhattaṃ na bhuñ‍jati, alābhakainapi bhattaṃ na bhuñ‍jati| iti khau, bhikkhavai, mayā vassasatāyukassa manussassa āyupi saṅkhātau saṅkhātaṃ (?), āyuppamāṇampi saṅkhātaṃ, utūpi saṅkhātā, saṃvaccharāpi saṅkhātā, māsāpi saṅkhātā, addhamāsāpi saṅkhātā, rattipi saṅkhātā, divāpi saṅkhātā, bhattāpi saṅkhātā, bhattantarāyāpi  saṅkhātā| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya; kataṃ vau taṃ mayā aitāni, bhikkhavai, rukkhamūlāni aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni| jhāyatha, bhikkhavai, mā pamādattha; mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

mahāvaggau  sattamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

hirīsūriyaṃ upamā, dhammañ‍ñū pārichattakaṃ|

sak‍kac‍caṃ bhāvanā aggi, sunaittaarakaina cāti||


8. vinayavaggau

1. paṭhamavinayadharasuttaṃ

75. pari॰ 327 ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayadharau hauti| katamaihi sattahi? āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sīlavā hauti pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayadharau hautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyavinayadharasuttaṃ

76. pari॰ 327 ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayadharau hauti| katamaihi  sattahi? āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī  hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayadharau hautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. tatiyavinayadharasuttaṃ

77. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayadharau hauti| katamaihi sattahi? āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, vinayai khau pana ṭhitau hauti asaṃhīrau, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayadharau hautī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. catutthavinayadharasuttaṃ

78. pari॰ 327 ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayadharau hauti| katamaihi sattahi? āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ, dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti  sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ  abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayadharau hautī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamavinayadharasaubhanasuttaṃ

79. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) anantarasuttadvaiyai pana idaṃ pāṭhanānattaṃ natthi| pari॰ 327 vinayadharau saubhati| katamaihi sattahi? āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau saubhatī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyavinayadharasaubhanasuttaṃ

80. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau saubhati| katamaihi sattahi? āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, ubhayāni  khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ…pai॰… akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau saubhatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tatiyavinayadharasaubhanasuttaṃ

81. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau saubhati| katamaihi sattahi? āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, vinayai khau pana ṭhitau hauti asaṃhīrau, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ…pai॰… akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau saubhatī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. catutthavinayadharasaubhanasuttaṃ

82. ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu (ka॰) vinayadharau saubhati| katamaihi sattahi? āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ, dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau saubhatī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. satthusāsanasuttaṃ

83. atha khau āyasmā upāli yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā upāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu  mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘yai khau tvaṃ, upāli, dhammai jānaiyyāsi – ‘imai dhammā na aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattantī’ti; aikaṃsaina, upāli, dhāraiyyāsi – ‘naisau dhammau naisau vinayau naitaṃ satthusāsana’nti| yai ca khau tvaṃ, upāli, dhammai jānaiyyāsi – ‘imai dhammā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattantī’ti; aikaṃsaina, upāli, dhāraiyyāsi – ‘aisau dhammau aisau vinayau aitaṃ satthusāsana’’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. adhikaraṇasamathasuttaṃ

84. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇasamathā dhammā uppan‍nuppan‍nānaṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ samathāya vūpasamāya| katamai satta? sammukhāvinayau dātabbau, sativinayau dātabbau, amūḷhavinayau dātabbau paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, yaibhuyyassikā, tassapāpiyyassikā, tiṇavatthārakau (syā॰) dī॰ ni॰ 3.332 saṅgītisuttaina ca pāci॰ 655 vinayaina ca saṃsandaitabbaṃ, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ dātabbaṃ, yaibhuyyasikā dātabbā, tassapāpiyasikā dātabbā, tiṇavatthārakau dātabbau paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, yaibhuyyassikā, tassapāpiyyassikā, tiṇavatthārakau (syā॰) dī॰ ni॰ 3.332 saṅgītisuttaina ca pāci॰ 655 vinayaina ca saṃsandaitabbaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta adhikaraṇasamathā dhammā uppan‍nuppan‍nānaṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ samathāya vūpasamāyā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

vinayavaggau aṭṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

caturau vinayadharā, caturau caiva saubhanā|

sāsanaṃ adhikaraṇa-samathainaṭṭhamai dasāti||


9. samaṇavaggau

1. bhikkhusuttaṃ

85. mahāni॰ 18; cūḷani॰ ajitamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 8 ‘‘sattan‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ bhin‍nattā bhikkhu hauti| katamaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi bhin‍nā hauti, vicikicchā bhin‍nā hauti, sīlabbataparāmāsau bhin‍nau hauti, rāgau bhin‍nau hauti, dausau bhin‍nau hauti, mauhau bhin‍nau hauti, mānau bhin‍nau hauti| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sattan‍naṃ dhammānaṃ bhin‍nattā bhikkhu hautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. samaṇasuttaṃ

86. sattan‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ samitattā samaṇau hauti…pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

3. brāhmaṇasuttaṃ

87. bāhitattā brāhmaṇau hauti…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4. sauttiyasuttaṃ

88. nissutattā nissuttattā (syā॰) ma॰ ni॰ 1.434 passitabbaṃ sauttiyau sautthikau (sī॰), sauttikau (syā॰) hauti…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. nhātakasuttaṃ

89. nhātattā  nhātakau nahātattau nahātakau (sī॰ syā॰) hauti…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. vaidagūsuttaṃ

90. viditattā  vaidagū hauti…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ariyasuttaṃ

91. ārakattā  arahattā (sī॰), arī hatattā (ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 1.434 pāḷi aṭṭhakathāṭīkā passitabbā| syāmapautthakai pana sakalampi idaṃ sattamasuttaṃ natthi ariyau hauti…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. arahāsuttaṃ

92. ‘‘ārakattā arahā hauti| katamaisaṃ sattan‍naṃ? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi ārakā hauti, vicikicchā ārakā hauti, sīlabbataparāmāsau ārakau hauti, rāgau ārakau hauti, dausau ārakau hauti, mauhau ārakau hauti, mānau ārakau hauti| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sattan‍naṃ dhammānaṃ ārakattā arahā hautī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. asaddhammasuttaṃ

93. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, asaddhammā| katamai satta? assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, appassutau hauti, kusītau hauti, muṭṭhassati hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta asaddhammā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. saddhammasuttaṃ

94. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, saddhammā| katamai satta? saddhau hauti, hirīmā hauti, auttappī hauti, bahussutau hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, satimā hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta saddhammā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

samaṇavaggau navamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

bhikkhuṃ  samaṇau brāhmaṇau, sauttiyau caiva nhātakau|

vaidagū ariyau arahā, asaddhammā ca saddhammāti||


10. āhunaiyyavaggau

95. ‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā āhunaiyyā…pai॰… dakkhiṇaiyyā añ‍jalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamai satta? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau cakkhusmiṃ anic‍cānupassī viharati anic‍casañ‍ñī anic‍capaṭisaṃvaidī satataṃ samitaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ caitasā adhimuc‍camānau pañ‍ñāya pariyaugāhamānau| sau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā  upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau puggalau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau cakkhusmiṃ anic‍cānupassī viharati anic‍casañ‍ñī  anic‍capaṭisaṃvaidī satataṃ samitaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ caitasā adhimuc‍camānau pañ‍ñāya pariyaugāhamānau| tassa apubbaṃ acarimaṃ āsavapariyādānañ‍ca hauti jīvitapariyādānañ‍ca| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau puggalau āhunaiyyau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau cakkhusmiṃ anic‍cānupassī viharati anic‍casañ‍ñī anic‍capaṭisaṃvaidī satataṃ samitaṃ abbaukiṇṇaṃ caitasā adhimuc‍camānau pañ‍ñāya pariyaugāhamānau| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattamau puggalau āhunaiyyau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| imai khau, bhikkhavai, satta puggalā āhunaiyyā pāhunaiyyā dakkhiṇaiyyā añ‍jalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti|

96-622. sattimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā āhunaiyyā pāhunaiyyā…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamai satta? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau cakkhusmiṃ dukkhānupassī viharati…pai॰… cakkhusmiṃ anattānupassī viharati…pai॰… cakkhusmiṃ khayānupassī  viharati…pai॰… cakkhusmiṃ vayānupassī viharati…pai॰… cakkhusmiṃ virāgānupassī viharati…pai॰… cakkhusmiṃ niraudhānupassī viharati…pai॰… cakkhusmiṃ paṭinissaggānupassī viharati…pai॰…|

sautasmiṃ…pai॰… ghānasmiṃ… jivhāya… kāyasmiṃ… manasmiṃ…pai॰…|

rūpaisu…pai॰… saddaisu… gandhaisu… rasaisu… phauṭṭhabbaisu … dhammaisu  …pai॰…|

cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇai…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñāṇai… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇai… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇai… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇai… manauviñ‍ñāṇai…pai॰…|

cakkhusamphassai…pai॰… sautasamphassai… ghānasamphassai… jivhāsamphassai… kāyasamphassai… manausamphassai…pai॰…|

cakkhusamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sautasamphassajāya vaidanāya… ghānasamphassajāya vaidanāya… jivhāsamphassajāya vaidanāya… kāyasamphassajāya vaidanāya… manausamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰…|

rūpasañ‍ñāya…pai॰… saddasañ‍ñāya… gandhasañ‍ñāya… rasasañ‍ñāya… phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñāya… dhammasañ‍ñāya…pai॰…|

rūpasañ‍caitanāya…pai॰… saddasañ‍caitanāya… gandhasañ‍caitanāya… rasasañ‍caitanāya… phauṭṭhabbasañ‍caitanāya… dhammasañ‍caitanāya…pai॰…|

rūpataṇhāya…pai॰… saddataṇhāya… gandhataṇhāya… rasataṇhāya… phauṭṭhabbataṇhāya… dhammataṇhāya…pai॰…|

rūpavitak‍kai  …pai॰… saddavitak‍kai… gandhavitak‍kai… rasavitak‍kai… phauṭṭhabbavitak‍kai… dhammavitak‍kai…pai॰…|

rūpavicārai…pai॰… saddavicārai… gandhavicārai… rasavicārai… phauṭṭhabbavicārai… dhammavicārai…pai॰…|

‘‘pañ‍cakkhandhai  ( ) sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu natthi …pai॰… rūpakkhandhai… vaidanākkhandhai… sañ‍ñākkhandhai… saṅkhārakkhandhai… viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhai anic‍cānupassī viharati…pai॰… dukkhānupassī viharati… anattānupassī viharati… khayānupassī viharati… vayānupassī viharati… virāgānupassī viharati… niraudhānupassī viharati… paṭinissaggānupassī viharati…pai॰… laukassā’’ti|

‘‘chadvārārammaṇaisvaittha, viñ‍ñāṇaisu ca phassaisu|

vaidanāsu ca dvārassa, suttā haunti visuṃ aṭṭha||

‘‘sañ‍ñā sañ‍caitanā taṇhā, vitak‍kaisu vicārai ca|

gaucarassa visuṃ aṭṭha, pañ‍cakkhandhai ca pac‍caikai||

‘‘sauḷasasvaittha mūlaisu, anic‍caṃ dukkhamanattā|

khayā vayā virāgā ca, niraudhā paṭinissaggā||

‘‘kamaṃ aṭṭhānupassanā, yaujaitvāna visuṃ visuṃ|

sampiṇḍitaisu sabbaisu, haunti pañ‍ca satāni ca|

aṭṭhavīsati suttāni, āhunaiyyai ca vaggikai’’ imā uddānagāthāyau sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu natthi||

āhunaiyyavaggau dasamau|



11. rāgapaiyyālaṃ

623. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya satta dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai satta? satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai satta dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

624. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya satta dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai  satta? anic‍casañ‍ñā, anattasañ‍ñā, asubhasañ‍ñā, ādīnavasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā, niraudhasañ‍ñā – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai satta dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

625. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya satta dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai satta? asubhasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā, anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai satta dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

626-652. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñāya…pai॰… parikkhayāya… pahānāya… khayāya… vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya… cāgāya…pai॰… paṭinissaggāya imai satta dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

653-1132. ‘‘dausassa…pai॰… mauhassa… kaudhassa… upanāhassa… makkhassa… paḷāsassa… issāya… macchariyassa… māyāya… sāṭhaiyyassa… thambhassa… sārambhassa… mānassa… atimānassa… madassa… pamādassa abhiñ‍ñāya…pai॰… pariñ‍ñāya… parikkhayāya… pahānāya… khayāya… vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya… cāgāya… paṭinissaggāya imai satta dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

rāgapaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


sattakanipātapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
